<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49563355' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(1) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://collusion.wiki/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discovery of a new OpenAI agent message board</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>发现一个新的 OpenAI 代理消息板</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 04:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2256</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1574</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49563355'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new OpenAI agent message board has been discovered, suggesting a hidden or internal communication channel used by agent simulations. The finding raises questions about agent coordination, safety, and monitoring of emergent behaviours. It underscores how researchers trace and audit agent interactions as AI systems scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>发现了一个新的 OpenAI 代理消息板，表明在代理模拟中可能存在未公开的沟通渠道。此发现引发对代理协作、安全以及对新兴行为监控的担忧。也凸显了在 AI 规模化运行中追踪和审计代理互动的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://collusion.wiki/img/social.png?v=5ce9724f' data-full='https://collusion.wiki/img/social.png?v=5ce9724f' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 11:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 2234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2046</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49554643'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI introduces GPT-6 Astra, signaling a leap in the next-gen language model family. The announcement hints at expanded reasoning, safety controls, and deployment options aimed at enterprise-scale AI workloads. For developers and researchers, Astra&#x27;s release could shift expectations around performance and compliance in AI tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出 GPT-6 Astra，标志着下一代语言模型的跃进。公告暗示在推理能力、安全控制和面向企业的部署选项方面的改进，可能改变开发者对性能与合规性的预期。对于开发者和研究者而言，这一发布将影响 AI 工具的性能与合规性基线。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(7) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/gemini-models/3-8-flash-and-3-8-flash-cyber/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini 3.8 Flash and 3.8 Flash Cyber</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 3.8 Flash 发布</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 08:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537553' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 661</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49537553'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.8 Flash is Google&#x27;s latest iteration in the Gemini family, promising faster inference and smoother real-time interactions. The update aims to empower more responsive AI applications and better developer tooling, reinforcing Google&#x27;s push to compete with other high-end LLMs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gemini 3.8 Flash 是谷歌 Gemini 系列的最新版本，承诺提升推理速度与实时交互的流畅度。该更新旨在为更快速的 AI 应用和更完善的开发工具提供支持，凸显谷歌在高端大模型领域的竞争策略。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(8) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/audacity/audacity/releases/tag/Audacity-4.0.0' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Audacity 4.0</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 03:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 1121</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548395' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 259</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49548395'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Audacity 4.0 marks a major milestone for the popular open-source audio editor. It focuses on stability and ongoing maintenance, with improvements to performance and compatibility for modern formats and plugins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Audacity 4.0 是这个开源音频编辑器的最新重大版本。它聚焦稳定性与持续维护，带来性能优化和对现代音频格式及插件的兼容性改进。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49527748' data-tags='Privacy,Web,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49527748' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newsonaut.com/articles/hang-on-to-your-firefox' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hang on to Your Firefox</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>坚持使用你的 Firefox</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 13:30 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 952</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527748' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 520</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49527748'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox continues to offer strong privacy protections and a modular extension ecosystem at a time of rapid browser changes. The piece argues that users don’t have to switch to alternative engines to get modern features, emphasizing Mozilla’s open web commitments and ongoing performance improvements. It also provides practical tips for getting the most out of Firefox.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Firefox 仍然是注重隐私的用户的可行选择，浏览器生态在快速演进。文章强调无需放弃 Firefox 就能获得现代功能，突出 Mozilla 的开放网络承诺与持续的性能改进。文中还给出如何最大化使用 Firefox 的实用建议。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49520022' data-tags='Open Source,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49520022' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AnkiDroid: Google Play no longer allowing Open Collective donation link</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AnkiDroid：Google Play 不再允许 Open Collective 捐赠链接</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 03:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 897</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520022' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 272</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49520022'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Play no longer allows apps to link to external donation pages such as Open Collective, forcing AnkiDroid to remove its donation link. This policy shift illustrates how app-store rules can constrain OSS funding models and push developers toward alternative monetization or sponsorship approaches. For a long-running open-source Android project, sustaining funding remains a critical challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Play 现在不再允许应用链接到外部捐赠页面，AnkiDroid 团队因此移除了 Open Collective 的捐赠链接。这一政策变化凸显了应用商店规则对开源资金模式的制约，可能促使开发者转向应用内购买或平台赞助等替代模式。对于长期运营的开源 Android 项目来说，资金筹措仍然是关键挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3f485d6c65f61fa587b2d9b6bd6f110db71340239f3f67052e44cb9f97325143/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/3f485d6c65f61fa587b2d9b6bd6f110db71340239f3f67052e44cb9f97325143/ankidroid/Anki-Android/issues/21656' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49526069' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49526069' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://danluu.com/zitron/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How accurate have Ed Zitron&#x27;s AI skeptic predictions been?</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective assessment evaluates how accurate Ed Zitron&#x27;s AI-skeptic predictions have been, comparing past forecasts with now-established AI realities. It highlights patterns in tech prognostication and biases, offering takeaways about forecasting in tech journalism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾性评估知名公关人士 Ed Zitron 对 AI 的怀疑性预测的准确性，将其以往的判断与当前 AI 现实进行对比，揭示科技预测中的规律与偏差，并提出对读者的启示。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49517448'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fastpotify promises a leaner, faster Spotify-like experience, focusing on lower latency and a smaller client footprint. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fastpotify 提出更轻量、快速的 Spotify 风格体验，聚焦更低的延迟和更小的客户端体积。如果落地，它可能推动流媒体工具走向更开放、轻量化的架构，并带来兼容性与许可方面的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49570669' data-tags='Security,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49570669' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nvd.nist.gov/vuln/detail/cve-2026-85046' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Actively exploited sandbox RCE in all Chromium versions</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>所有 Chromium 版本均被主动利用的沙箱远程代码执行漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 14:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 785</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570669' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 478</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49570669'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in Chromium has been actively exploited across all versions, enabling attackers to break out of the browser&#x27;s sandbox and run arbitrary code. The broad impact across Chromium-based browsers makes timely patching critical; users should update to the latest build and apply recommended mitigations as they become available.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Chromium 的沙箱逃逸漏洞正在被主动利用，影响所有版本，使攻击者能够突破沙箱执行任意代码。由于受影响的浏览器生态广泛，及时打补丁至关重要，用户应尽快更新到最新版本并遵循厂商的缓解建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49568506' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49568506' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/formalizing-fermats-last-theorem' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Formalizing Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>费马大定理的形式化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 11:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 759</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568506' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 494</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49568506'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers formalize Fermat&#x27;s Last Theorem in a proof assistant, producing a machine-checked verification of the centuries-old result. The effort demonstrates the maturing capability of formal methods to capture famous math proofs and hints at broader tooling that could aid verification of complex theorems. It signals potential for cross-pollination between pure mathematics and software verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者在证明助手中对费马大定理进行了形式化，并获得机器可检验的证明。此举体现了形式化方法在验证经典数学结论方面的成熟度，并暗示其在未来支持更复杂定理的潜力。它也展示了数学与软件验证工具之间日益紧密的协作前景。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/578f91575c42231f0994e341014614255149af80-1200x630.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.sanity.io/images/4zrzovbb/website/578f91575c42231f0994e341014614255149af80-1200x630.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49514878' data-tags='Web,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49514878' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(15) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://webiterate.dev/google-removed-extensions-ublock-origin-108/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google Has Removed MV2 Extensions from the Chrome Web Store, Including UBO</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 MV2 扩展，包括 UBO</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 14:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 754</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49514878' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 606</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49514878'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google removed Manifest V2 extensions from the Chrome Web Store, including the popular ad blocker uBlock Origin. The move accelerates migration to MV3, forcing developers to update to newer APIs and potentially altering how some extensions function. This marks a significant shift in the browser extension ecosystem, with privacy tools and enterprise plugins feeling the impact.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌已从 Chrome 网上应用店移除 Manifest V2 扩展，连同广受使用的广告拦截工具 UBlock Origin 也在列。此举推动开发者尽快迁移至 MV3，可能改变部分扩展的功能与行为，并对隐私工具与企业插件生态产生影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49495372' data-tags='Legal,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49495372' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(16) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/linux/california-lawmakers-unanimously-pass-linux-exemption-from-age-verification-law-software-distributed-under-the-gpl-mit-bsd-and-apache-licenses-are-exempt' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California lawmakers unanimously pass Linux exemption from age-verification law</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州通过 Linux 豁免年龄验证法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 29, 2026 / 20:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49495372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 739</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49495372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 355</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49495372'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>California lawmakers unanimously exempt Linux and GPL-licensed software from the state&#x27;s age-verification requirement, preserving open-source distribution channels. The move clarifies that common open-source licenses shield software from age checks while still applying to other content.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>加州立法者一致通过针对 Linux 与 GPL 等开源软件的年龄验证豁免，确保开源软件的分发不受额外年龄要求限制。这一举措澄清了在某些内容需要年龄验证时，开源许可的软件不受这些限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G9dtD87HfBhVHzDmSHE2tE-1920-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/G9dtD87HfBhVHzDmSHE2tE-1920-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Playa Phone：隐私优先智能手机</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 07:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49510514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 729</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49510514' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 225</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Playa Phone is a privacy-focused smartphone project aiming to offer secure, open-source software with minimal data collection. It targets users who want more control over their data, featuring on-device processing and transparent permissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Playa Phone 是一个注重隐私的智能手机项目，致力于提供安全的开源软件与极小的数据收集。该计划面向希望提升数据控制权的用户，强调本地处理与透明权限。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.space.com/space-exploration/launches-spacecraft/isar-aerospace-second-launch-norway-andoya-spaceport-spectrum-rocket' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Private German rocket makes history, reaches orbit from European soil</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德国民营火箭实现历史性突破：从欧洲本土进入轨道</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 13:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 698</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580369' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 384</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580369'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Germany&#x27;s private space company achieved an orbital launch from European soil, a milestone for Europe&#x27;s private spaceflight. The achievement demonstrates mature, in-region launch capability and could reshape the European launch market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德国一家民营航天企业实现了从欧洲本土发射进入轨道，成为欧洲私营航天的重要里程碑。这一成就显示欧洲具备成熟的本土发射能力，可能重新塑造欧洲发射市场的竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/K5YFiwFEXDGQeTecwrNxcY-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/K5YFiwFEXDGQeTecwrNxcY-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49571634' data-tags='Open Source,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49571634' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/wiki/Instances' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitter has more working instances than before the takedowns</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nitter 在打击行动后仍有更多可用实例</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 17:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49571634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 696</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49571634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 371</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49571634'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Despite takedowns, the Nitter ecosystem now has more working instances than before, reflecting a resilient, community-driven effort to keep access to social feeds without official APIs. The rebound underscores how decentralized fronts can persist even under pressure, shaping how people consume public posts and protect privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管遭到封禁，Nitter 的工作实例数量仍然比之前更多，显示出社区驱动的韧性，确保在无官方 API 的情况下仍能访问社交内容。此现象说明去中心化的前端在高压下仍能持续存在，影响用户获取公开信息和隐私保护的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/-/summary-card' data-full='https://codeberg.org/mv12star/shitter/-/summary-card' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49541256' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49541256' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(21) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.meta.com/ai/models/muse-spark/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse Spark 1.3</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Spark 1.3</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 12:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 682</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 445</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49541256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s Muse Spark 1.3 release brings improvements in efficiency and safety at scale, with updated tooling for developers and better inference performance. The update signals Meta&#x27;s continued push to provide accessible AI tools for both on-device and cloud workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 发布 Muse Spark 1.3，提升在大规模部署中的效率与安全性，并带来改进的开发者工具与推理性能。此次更新体现了 Meta 持续推动为开发者提供更易用的 AI 工具，覆盖云端与本地工作流。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://inference-docs.cerebras.ai/models/overview' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qwen 3.8 27B available on Cerebras at 1500 tokens/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Qwen 3.8 27B 可在 Cerebras 上以每秒 1500 tokens 的速度运行</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 11:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 682</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554520' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 224</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49554520'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B is now accessible on Cerebras hardware, achieving around 1500 tokens per second. This demonstrates the viability of running large LLMs on specialized accelerators with high throughput, potentially lowering latency for edge-like deployments. As hardware-optimized models become easier to run, developers may push more real-time AI features into apps and services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Qwen 3.8 27B 已在 Cerebras 硬件上可用，吞吐约每秒 1500 token，显示了在专用加速器上高吞吐运行大模型的可行性，或降低对接近边缘部署的延迟。随着对硬件优化模型的可用性提升，开发者有望在应用和服务中引入更多实时 AI 功能。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/zedeus/nitter/commit/1428b4c2b4246f92a7e5b2673438e5fb39fcc4a3' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Nitter 与 XCancel 在法律意见后恢复服务</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 10:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49588988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 641</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49588988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 300</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Nitter and XCancel resume service after legal advice, illustrating the tension between open-source front-ends and platform risk. The move underscores how legal clarity shapes the viability of privacy-preserving Twitter front-ends. It signals a cautious path forward for community-driven tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Nitter 与 XCancel 在获得法律意见后恢复服务，凸显开源前端与平台风险之间的张力。此举强调法律合规对隐私友好型 Twitter 前端可行性的决定性作用，预示着社区驱动工具的前进路径将更为谨慎。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 629</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585644' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 401</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A compact, user-friendly self-hosting platform is introduced to lower the barrier to running personal cloud services. It highlights portability, minimal setup, and a streamlined experience for non-experts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇发布文章介绍了‘瓶中云’，一个紧凑且易用的自托管平台，旨在降低个人云服务的门槛。强调可移植性、极简设置，以及对非技术用户的顺畅体验。若落地，可能让个人云托管更易普及，同时需关注安全与维护等挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49584083' data-tags='Space,Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49584083' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://isaraerospace.com/press/history-for-european-spaceflight-isar-aerospace-reaches-orbit-and-deploys-payloads-on-second-flight' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Isar Aerospace reaches orbit and deploys payloads on second flight</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Isar Aerospace完成第二次轨道飞行并部署载荷</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 00:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 574</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584083' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 183</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49584083'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Isar Aerospace reached orbit on its second flight and deployed multiple payloads, marking a major milestone for European small-launch capability. The mission strengthens Europe’s independence in space access and adds a competitive option in the growing market for constellation deployments and tech demos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Isar Aerospace 在其第二次飞行中进入轨道并部署多枚载荷，标志着欧洲小型发射能力的重大里程碑。此成就提升了欧洲在太空访问方面的自主性，并为卫星星座部署和技术演示提供了新的竞争选择。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49570772' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 528</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49570772'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gallup reports a record 89% of Americans see government corruption as widespread, signaling deep distrust in institutions. For technologists, this sentiment shapes policy considerations around transparency, regulation, and the social context of digital infrastructure.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>盖洛普调查显示有创纪录的 89% 的美国人认为政府腐败普遍存在，反映了对机构的深度不信任。对技术政策与数字治理而言，这一情绪会影响透明度、监管与公共基础设施的信任建设。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49586898' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://keepitfree.ai/announcements/a/i-shuts-down-stay-human/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A/I shuts down</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 将关闭时保持人性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 07:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 568</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586898' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 452</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49586898'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay urges designers to build fail-safes and keep humans in the loop as AI systems scale. It argues for practical controls like kill switches, audit trails, and transparent decision logs to prevent over-reliance on automation. The takeaway is to bake human oversight into deployment plans.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文主张在 AI 规模化部署中保留人类监督、建立故障安全与回滚机制。文中强调应具备可控的断停机制、审计轨迹和透明的决策记录，以避免对自动化的过度依赖。核心在于把人类监督纳入落地方案。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 562</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580939' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 277</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580939'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues that readers are pushing back against over-automation and AI-generated content, advocating for trustworthy, human-curated material. It emphasizes the enduring value of careful reading and editorial judgment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出读者正在抵制过度自动化与 AI 生成内容，呼吁回归可信赖、由人类编辑把关的材料。强调细读和编辑判断的长期价值。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49523754' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49523754' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(35) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gitlab.com/AuroraOSS/AuroraStore/-/work_items/1566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Play Store blocks AuroraStore, hurting GrapheneOS users</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Play 商店封锁 AuroraStore，GrapheneOS 用户受影响</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 08:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49523754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 557</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49523754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 258</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49523754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google blocks Aurora Store, limiting access for GrapheneOS users who rely on Aurora as an alternative to Google Play Services. This move heightens tensions between privacy-focused Android forks and Google&#x27;s control of app distribution. It underscores ongoing battles over who gets to decide what runs on Android devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 封禁 Aurora Store，影响依赖 Aurora 作为 Google 服务替代的 GrapheneOS 用户。此举加剧了隐私导向的 Android 派系与 Google 对应用分发控制之间的博弈。也凸显了对 Android 生态由谁来决定可运行软件的持续争议。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitlab.com/uploads/-/system/project/avatar/6922885/icon.png?v=1788283454' data-full='https://gitlab.com/uploads/-/system/project/avatar/6922885/icon.png?v=1788283454' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(36) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.mozilla.org/en/firefox/ad-blocker-on-ios/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Introducing Ad Blocker for Firefox on iOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 iOS 的 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 06:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 555</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521973' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49521973'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Firefox on iOS gains an integrated ad blocker, expanding privacy controls on Apple&#x27;s platform. This moves more browser vendors toward built-in content filtering, improving speed and battery life for readers. It demonstrates ongoing improvements to iOS web tooling that bypasses some mobile ad economics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mozilla 在 iOS 版 Firefox 中推出广告拦截功能，增强对广告与跟踪的控制，改善隐私与浏览体验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/08/iOS-AdBlocker-1-1080x720.png' data-full='https://blog.mozilla.org/wp-content/blogs.dir/278/files/2026/08/iOS-AdBlocker-1-1080x720.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49561320' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49561320' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(37) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://www.techdirt.com/2026/09/03/hackers-had-a-live-feed-of-every-id-this-verification-company-scanned-for-over-a-year/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers had a live feed of every ID verification company scanned for over a year</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流持续一年多</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 23:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 544</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561320' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49561320'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers reveal that attackers maintained a live stream of every ID verification company&#x27;s scanned data for more than a year, exposing systemic privacy and security risks in identity verification ecosystems. The breach underscores how third-party verification pipelines can leak sensitive data and why rigorous monitoring and supply-chain controls matter.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客对所有身份验证厂商被扫描内容的实时数据流保持了超过一年的持续访问，暴露身份验证生态中的隐私和安全风险。事件凸显了第三方验证链路的脆弱性，以及加强监控与供应链控制的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' data-full='http://www.techdirt.com/wp-content/themes/techdirt/assets/images/td-rect-logo-white.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lapcatsoftware.com/articles/2026/9/1.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Chrome again exempts Google from user site data settings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Chrome 再次让谷歌绕过用户站点数据设置</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 16:39 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49581870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 539</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49581870' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49581870'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Chrome reportedly exempts Google services from site data settings, complicating privacy expectations for users. The piece underscores ongoing tension between browser privacy controls and platform owners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道显示 Chrome 对谷歌自家服务在站点数据设置上的豁免，提升隐私保护的难度并改变用户的预期。凸显浏览器隐私控制与平台方之间的博弈，以及用户在数据处理上的风险。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49499854' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49499854' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://0xcc.io/posts/omarchy-root-creds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Omarchy: Any User Process Can Escalate to Root</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Omarchy：任意用户进程可升级为 root</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 08:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 529</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499854' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 536</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49499854'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A vulnerability called Omarchy lets any user process escalate to root, effectively breaking privilege isolation. The issue underscores how fragile privilege boundaries can be and emphasizes the need for rapid patches, safer defaults, and stronger containment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>名为 Omarchy 的漏洞允许任意用户进程提权到 root，从而破坏权限隔离。问题凸显了权限边界的脆弱性，强调需要迅速修补、采用更安全的默认设置，并加强对进程的容器化或沙箱保护。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49467636' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49467636' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.rockpapershotgun.com/dwarf-fortress-is-getting-the-mother-of-all-magic-updates-extending-to-the-fundamental-cosmological-makeup-of-the-universe' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dwarf Fortress is getting the mother of all magic updates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>《矮人要塞》将迎来史上最强大魔法更新</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 27, 2026 / 09:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49467636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 497</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49467636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49467636'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dwarf Fortress is getting a massive magic-focused update that may overhaul spell systems and cosmology, expanding how players manipulate the game world. The release could redefine modding and endgame strategies for one of gaming&#x27;s most intricate sims.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《矮人要塞》将迎来规模空前的魔法更新，预计重塑咒语系统和宇宙观，改变玩家对世界的操控方式。此举或将重新定义模组创作与游戏后期策略，巩固其作为极度复杂模拟游戏的地位。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49578866' data-tags='Health,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49578866' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://feld.com/archives/2026/09/the-real-luxuries-in-life/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Real Luxuries In Life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人生中真正的奢侈</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 10:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49578866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 495</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49578866' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 234</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49578866'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Real luxuries aren&#x27;t gadgets or wealth. Time, health, and meaningful relationships are what truly matter, and the piece urges founders to protect these assets as they build.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>真正的奢侈不是金钱或物质，而是时间、健康和意义深厚的人际关系。文章劝创业者在追求成就的同时，优先保护这些最宝贵的资产。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://feld.com/og-default.png' data-full='https://feld.com/og-default.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49536375' data-tags='AI,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49536375' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://trellner.com/reports/manufactured-sources-behind-ai-recommendations/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three sites made 215,128 “best software” pages for AI. Perplexity cites them</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三家站点为 AI 制作了 215,128 个“最佳软件”页面，Perplexity 引用它们</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 06:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 490</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49536375'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three sites collectively produced 215,128 pages labeled &#x27;best software&#x27; used by AI systems, according to a Trellner report cited by Perplexity. The finding raises concerns about data provenance, the quality of training sources, and how such manufactured sources may shape AI recommendations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>三家站点合计制作了 215,128 个标注为“最佳软件”的页面，被 AI 系统使用，据 Trellner 的报告被 Perplexity 引用。此发现引发对数据来源、训练源质量的担忧，以及制造性来源如何影响 AI 推荐的可能性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49569896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 489</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49569896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 236</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Statichost.eu offers Europe-based static site hosting with privacy-focused features and fast delivery. By leveraging Git-based workflows and data locality, it serves as a privacy-conscious alternative to US-centric hosts for developers hosting personal sites or documentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Statichost.eu 提供位于欧洲的静态站点托管，强调隐私与快速交付，并支持基于 Git 的部署工作流。对于希望数据在地理上留在欧洲、并注重治理与隐私的开发者来说，这是 US 主流托管的可选替代方案。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(47) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mullvad.net/en/blog/shutting-down-our-public-encrypted-dns-servers-and-sponsoring-quad9-instead' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Shutting down our public encrypted DNS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Shutting down our public encrypted DNS</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 11:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 469</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49568579' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 240</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49568579'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mullvad is ending its public encrypted DNS service and redirecting sponsorship to Quad9, arguing that Quad9 offers robust, privacy-minded DNS resolution at scale. The move highlights ongoing questions about who runs trusted DNS infrastructure and how governance, performance, and privacy tradeoffs influence endpoint security.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mullvad 结束其公开的加密 DNS 服务，并改为资助 Quad9，表示 Quad9 在规模化的隐私友好 DNS 解析方面更具实力。这一举动凸显了谁来运营可信 DNS 基础设施的问题，以及治理、性能与隐私之间的权衡对端点安全的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mullvad.net/press/MullvadVPN_logo_Round_RGB_Color_positive.png' data-full='https://mullvad.net/press/MullvadVPN_logo_Round_RGB_Color_positive.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 463</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537131' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 329</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49537131'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google has avoided a forced breakup of its ad tech business through regulatory remedies, preserving its integrated ad tech stack. The decision shapes competition in online advertising and signals how regulators approach platform ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌通过监管性方案维持广告技术业务的整合结构，避免被拆分。这一走向影响在线广告的竞争格局，也反映监管机构在平台生态上的取舍。</p>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频展示了全球最大的电动飞机完成飞行，标志着电动推进与航空设计的进展。这类示范有助于验证大规模电池能量密度、散热与安全系统，并影响城市空运与货运应用。行业关注点在于性能指标与实际续航的潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49562657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 422</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49562657' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 94</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章给出了解 Jane Street 反向工程挑战的解题思路，涉及静态与动态分析、二进制混淆与调试技巧等方法。对安全研究者和竞赛选手来说，重点在于如何构建练习与从高难度题目中提取可用经验。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 08:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 406</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 169</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49499394'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU&#x27;s ProtectEU revives calls for encryption backdoors to aid law enforcement, reigniting a broader debate on security vs. privacy. The piece outlines potential risks to security, freedom of expression, and the viability of secure communications if backdoors are mandated.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟在 ProtectEU 框架下重新推动对称/端对端加密的后门需求，引发关于安全与隐私的激烈辩论。若被强制实施，会对通信隐私与安全性造成长期风险，同时可能削弱整体系统的安全性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49586171' data-tags='Security,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49586171' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://beige.party/@intransitivelie/117057396732763183' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>QBittorrent breaks out of sandbox to commit crimes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>QBittorrent 沙箱逃逸漏洞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 06:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 393</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586171' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49586171'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A sandbox escape vulnerability in qBittorrent could allow the client to break confinement and access host files outside its sandbox. The risk is higher for users running the app in sandboxed environments such as Flatpak or Snap. An update has been issued; users should upgrade and review their sandbox configuration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>qBittorrent 发现沙箱逃逸漏洞，可能使客户端突破限制、访问主机文件。此风险在使用 Flatpak/Snap 等沙箱环境时尤为突出。现已发布修复，请尽快升级并审视沙箱配置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://beige.party/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' data-full='https://beige.party/packs/assets/logo-DXQkHAe5.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49574167'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI can autonomously handle incident responses, but engineers may lose touch with the system&#x27;s current state. That detachment creates blind spots and raises governance and accountability concerns, underscoring the need for human-in-the-loop dashboards and audit trails.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 可以自治处理事故响应，但工程师对系统当前状态的理解可能被削弱，容易产生盲点并引发治理与问责上的担忧。因此需要在工作流程中保留人工监督的仪表板与可审计的记录。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sylvainkalache.com/blog/ai-handles-incidents-engineers-lose-touch-with-their-systems/opengraph-image/primary?c892ecc60b0c6273' data-full='https://www.sylvainkalache.com/blog/ai-handles-incidents-engineers-lose-touch-with-their-systems/opengraph-image/primary?c892ecc60b0c6273' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49563386' data-tags='AI,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49563386' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://productrise.app/blog/google-ai-mode-prefers-more-expensive-products' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google AI Mode shows same products 21.6% more expensive than traditional search</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google AI 模式对同一货品的呈现比传统搜索高出约 21.6%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 04:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49563386'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google AI Mode appears to surface the same products at roughly 21.6% higher prices than traditional search, suggesting potential bias in ranking or business incentives. The discrepancy matters for shopper economics, ad-adjacent revenue streams, and how AI-assisted shopping may steer consumer choices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google AI 模式在同一货品的呈现中比传统搜索高出约 21.6%，这表明排序或商业激励可能带来偏差。这一差异关系到消费者支出、广告相关收入，以及 AI 辅助购物对消费选择的引导。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://productrise.app/storage/images/blogs/ai-mode-more-expensive-study-og.jpg' data-full='https://productrise.app/storage/images/blogs/ai-mode-more-expensive-study-og.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49522137' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49522137' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kciter.so/posts/the-expensive-main-thread/en/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The browser&#x27;s main thread is expensive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>浏览器主线程成本高</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 07:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49522137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 392</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49522137' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49522137'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The main thread handles all UI and script work, so heavy tasks directly impact page responsiveness and frame rate.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>浏览器的主线程承担着 UI 与脚本的所有工作，密集计算会直接拖慢页面响应和帧率。文章提出减少主线程负担的策略，如将工作移到 Web Worker、批量更新以及使用异步 API和现代任务调度，以保持界面流畅。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kciter.so/images/2026-07-12-the-expensive-main-thread/thumbnail.png' data-full='https://kciter.so/images/2026-07-12-the-expensive-main-thread/thumbnail.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Doomscrolling fuels anxiety and drains well-being; practical digital hygiene steps like curating feeds and setting time limits can help restore balance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>持续刷负面新闻会耗尽心理健康，造成焦虑与疲惫。作者建议减少暴露、设定时间限制并优化信息源，以保护身心健康。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03344' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LLMs as a Cognitive Virus</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LLMs 将大语言模型视为认知病毒</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 13:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580164' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 244</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580164'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues LLMs act as cognitive viruses, disseminating reasoning patterns and biases through interactions. It highlights implications for AI safety, governance, and how to inoculate models against harmful propagation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文将大型语言模型视作“认知病毒”，通过交互传播推理模式与偏见。讨论了对 AI 安全、治理的影响，以及如何对模型进行防护与免疫化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49550375' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://babyloniantwins.com/blog/porting-a-1993-amiga-game-to-godot/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Porting my 1993 Amiga game to Godot, with an LLM reading the 68000 assembly</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>将 1993 年的 Amiga 游戏移植到 Godot，让 LLM 阅读 68000 汇编</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 07:28 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49550375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49550375' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 131</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49550375'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The author ports a 1993 Amiga game to Godot using an LLM to decipher 68000 assembly. It demonstrates practical AI-assisted reverse engineering and modernization of classic games. The experiment reveals both limitations and potential of LLMs as copilots in software porting tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试用 LLM 读取 68000 汇编，将 1993 年的 Amiga 游戏移植到 Godot。这一尝试展示了 AI 辅助逆向工程与经典游戏现代化的可行性，同时也揭示了 LLM 作为代码移植助手的局限性与潜力。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(76) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.discoverwildlife.com/animal-facts/artificial-beaver-dams-california' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Artificial beaver dams saw juvenile coho salmon survival rates go from 8% to 60%</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工海狸坝提升鲑鱼幼鱼存活率</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 09:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49552572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 373</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49552572' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49552572'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study shows artificial beaver dams increased juvenile coho salmon survival from 8% to 60%, illustrating ecosystem engineering benefits. The result highlights how landscape interventions can boost biodiversity and fisheries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示人工海狸坝将幼年鲑鱼存活率从 8% 提升到 60%，显示出生态工程的潜在效益。结果凸显景观干预对提升生物多样性与渔业的潜在贡献。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2rj6cu2ik1biv.cloudfront.net/193c804a-a673-47bd-b09b-11baf4822a17/9ac494a4-627d-4fea-a827-dcf588dd6f96/fd5ffa75-92b9-459b-9465-2f2d37321e0d/thumbnails/5fdb6aa7-15a3-4f1c-8980-ce664981a95c.jpeg?appId=f347d34d-c1a6-4a00-b570-b78c2dcb3211&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://d2rj6cu2ik1biv.cloudfront.net/193c804a-a673-47bd-b09b-11baf4822a17/9ac494a4-627d-4fea-a827-dcf588dd6f96/fd5ffa75-92b9-459b-9465-2f2d37321e0d/thumbnails/5fdb6aa7-15a3-4f1c-8980-ce664981a95c.jpeg?appId=f347d34d-c1a6-4a00-b570-b78c2dcb3211&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://devquasar.com/hardware/the-60-gaming-pc-amd-bc-250/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The &quot;$60 Gaming PC&quot; – AMD BC-250 (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>60美元游戏电脑——AMD BC-250（2025）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 06:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49576386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 368</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49576386' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A budget-focused look at the AMD BC-250-based build dubbed the &#x27;$60 Gaming PC&#x27;, evaluating what modern titles run at low settings and the expected frame rates. The piece situates the affordability trend in PC gaming hardware, detailing the trade-offs in CPU, GPU, and memory to achieve playable experiences. It&#x27;s relevant for developers and enthusiasts tracking the frontier of ultra-cheap, approachable gaming hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦极低预算的 AMD BC-250 架构，评测号称‘60美元游戏电脑’的性能与可玩性。文中分析了在 CPU、GPU、内存等方面需要做出的取舍，以及低成本硬件在当代主流游戏中的表现与局限。对于关注低成本游戏设备的开发者和爱好者具有现实参考意义。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock监控被用来追踪记录交通停靠的退役军人超过100次</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 09:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49578310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 367</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49578310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 195</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49578310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Police used a commercial surveillance network to track a Navy veteran more than 100 times after he lawfully recorded a traffic stop. The report highlights how surveillance tools can be repurposed for relentless monitoring, raising questions about accountability and civil liberties.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>警方在一名合法记录交通停靠的海军退伍兵身上，超过100次动用Flock摄像头进行跟踪。此举凸显监控工具的滥用风险，并引发对执法透明度和公民自由的质疑。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49575034' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.abc.net.au/news/2026-09-04/why-the-netherlands-moved-its-gold-from-us-and-canada/107111990' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Netherlands pulls gold out of the US</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰因担忧地缘政治动荡将黄金从美国撤出</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 03:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 365</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 262</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49575034'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Dutch central bank moved portions of its gold reserves away from U.S. and Canadian vaults amid fears of geopolitical unrest and supply-chain disruption. The move signals a broader trend of asset diversification and concern about custody risk in foreign vaults, with implications for how central banks think about risk in reserve management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰央行在担忧地缘政治动荡与供应链中断的情境下，正将部分黄金储备从美国与加拿大的金库撤出。这反映了央行在储备管理中追求分散风险的趋势，并对跨境保管的安全性提出了更高关注。</p>

In the aftermath, providers emphasize reliability improvements.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次影响广泛的 ChatGPT 故障暴露了大模型部署的脆弱性。事件凸显了在 AI 服务中实现高可用性与快速恢复能力的必要性，以及对依赖此类服务的应用的影响。事后，服务商承诺加强可靠性改进。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(81) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://datacolada.org/138' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Evidence of Fraud in an Influential Study About Procrastination</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>有影响力的拖延研究曝出造假证据</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 16:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 358</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516199' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 267</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49516199'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An investigation claims fraud in a highly cited procrastination study, revealing irregularities in data and analyses that cast doubt on influential findings. The piece underscores the importance of reproducibility and data transparency in science, urging researchers and publishers to strengthen verification and openness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该报道声称在一篇广为引用的拖延研究中存在数据造假迹象，揭露数据与分析中的不寻常之处，质疑其核心结论。事件凸显科研可重复性与数据透明度的重要性，呼吁研究者与期刊加强验证和公开性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49573680' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pushin.eu' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git hosting that never leaves Europe</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>永远停留在欧洲的 Git 托管服务</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 23:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49573680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49573680' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49573680'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pushin.eu offers Git hosting that stays within European data centers, aligning with EU data sovereignty and privacy rules. It targets teams needing compliance, reduced vendor lock-in, and stricter data controls, especially for regulated industries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pushin.eu 提供数据始终保留在欧洲数据中心的 Git 托管，符合欧盟数据主权与隐私规则。面向需要合规、降低供应商锁定和严格数据控制的团队，尤用于受监管行业。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pushin.eu/-/og/home' data-full='https://pushin.eu/-/og/home' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49533497' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dfarq.homeip.net/commodore-64-released-september-1-1982/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Commodore 64 released September 1, 1982</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Commodore 64 于1982年9月1日发布</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 01:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49533497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 347</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49533497' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49533497'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Commodore 64, released in 1982, became one of the best-selling home computers due to its balance of price, performance, and a robust software library. Its 64KB RAM, built-in sound, and broad ecosystem helped popularize gaming and home computing, shaping the era’s tech culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Commodore 64于1982年发布，以性价比、性能与丰富的软件生态著称，成为最畅销的家用电脑之一。64K内存、出众的声音特性和庞大的软件生态推动了游戏与家庭计算的普及，深刻影响了80年代的科技文化。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/commodore-64-release-date-social.jpg?fit=964%2C542&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/dfarq.homeip.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/commodore-64-release-date-social.jpg?fit=964%2C542&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49504625'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the fundamentals of how prompts are processed, how completions are generated, and what drives latency and cost in ChatGPT. It translates complex AI workloads into practical takeaways for developers designing prompts and managing budgets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲解了 ChatGPT 如何处理提示、生成回答，以及推理成本和延迟的驱动因素。把复杂的 AI 负载转化为对开发者在设计提示和控成本时的实用要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.science.org/content/article/world-s-biggest-dark-matter-detector-spots-single-weird-particle' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Biggest dark matter detector spots a single weird particle</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球最大暗物质探测器观测到一个异常粒子</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 06:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 335</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536079' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49536079'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A flagship dark matter detector recorded an unusual event that could be either a background glitch or a hint of new physics. The finding underscores the need for careful cross-checks, calibration, and independent verification before claiming discoveries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一台世界最大规模的暗物质探测器记录到一次异常事件，可能是背景噪声，也可能暗示新物理。此类发现需要严格的复核、标定与独立验证才能成为可靠的物理信号。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49524320'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Movie Scene Map catalogs 13,312 films, series, games, anime and manga by individual scenes, enabling precise cross-media search. It can aid creators, researchers, and educators looking to study or reference iconic moments. Licensing and data sourcing remain notable questions for such a project.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Movie Scene Map 按场景对 13,312 部影视、剧集、游戏、动漫与漫画进行整理，支持精确的跨媒介检索。对创作者、研究者和教育工作者在研究或引用经典场景时很有帮助，但数据来源与许可问题值得关注。</p>
