<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49563784' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49563784' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(169) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/gadgets/2026/09/nearly-impossible-how-fairphone-built-the-ethical-repairable-fairphone-gen-6/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Fairphone built the Fairphone Gen 6+</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fairphone Gen 6+ 的设计与制造</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 05:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 195</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49563784' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49563784'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fairphone intentionally designs for repairability and ethical sourcing, tackling modularity, spare-part availability, and long-term software support to extend device life. The Gen 6+ embodies improvements in repairability, transparency, and reuse, but also faces real-world constraints like component obsolescence and cost pressures that complicate a truly consumer-friendly repair experience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fairphone 将可维修性和伦理采购作为核心，通过模块化设计、易更换部件和长期软件支持来延长设备寿命。Gen 6+ 在可维修性与透明度方面有所提升，但部件老化和成本压力等现实挑战仍制约其实现真正的“人人可修”的目标。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Fairphone-6-8-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Fairphone-6-8-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49573455' data-tags='Finance,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49573455' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(170) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cvgyn8q8gqxo' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are European countries moving their gold out of North America?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何欧洲国家把黄金从北美运出？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 22:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49573455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49573455' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 303</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49573455'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European nations are shifting portions of their gold reserves away from North American custody to European vaults, citing diversification, security, and geopolitical risk. The move tests gold-market dynamics and custody strategies at the central-bank level, with potential implications for reserve diversification trends.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲各国正在将部分黄金储备从北美托管转移至欧洲金库，原因包括分散风险、提升安全性和地缘政治考虑。此举可能影响黄金市场的托管安排与央行储备配置的长期趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/69ed/live/1f74ae20-a870-11f1-bbad-43eaf67b94b6.jpg' data-full='https://ichef.bbci.co.uk/news/1024/branded_news/69ed/live/1f74ae20-a870-11f1-bbad-43eaf67b94b6.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49564555' data-tags='Privacy,Security,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49564555' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/sep/04/military-disables-phone-ad-trackers' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Military disables ad trackers on troops&#x27; phones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美军为官兵手机禁用广告追踪器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 06:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49564555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 193</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49564555' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49564555'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Months after mounting privacy concerns, the Department of Defense has disabled ad-tracking features on service members&#x27; phones to curb data collection and targeting. The step signals a broader push to reduce consumer-tracking risk on government devices, potentially influencing contractors and vendors who supply mobile apps to the military.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在日益突出的隐私担忧之际，美国国防部已禁止在官兵手机上使用广告追踪功能，以减少数据收集与定向投放。此举体现出政府设备上降低跟踪风险的趋势，或将影响为军方提供移动应用的厂商与承包商。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/28a6d4d584aaee15675af89663fbbe5829d4ad4b/399_0_3990_3192/master/3990.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4104ffbce74c1feb8da3cae643abcc81' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/28a6d4d584aaee15675af89663fbbe5829d4ad4b/399_0_3990_3192/master/3990.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=4104ffbce74c1feb8da3cae643abcc81' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49565693' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49565693' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://support.google.com/mail/answer/22370?hl=en' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gmail to end support for &quot;Send as&quot; for third-party addresses, such as @yahoo.com</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gmail 将不再支持第三方邮箱地址的“发送为”功能（如 @yahoo.com）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 08:03 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49565693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49565693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 130</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49565693'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gmail will discontinue the &#x27;Send as&#x27; feature for third-party addresses, forcing users to either set up aliasing through Google accounts or switch to sending from the sender&#x27;s own servers. The change reduces potential spoofing and phishing vectors, but may disrupt workflows for users who rely on multi-domain aliases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Gmail 将不再支持来自第三方邮箱地址的“发送为”功能，用户需要改用 Google 账户的邮箱别名或自家服务器发送。此举旨在降低伪装和钓鱼风险，但可能打乱依赖跨域别名的工作流。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/p3J-ZSPOLtuBBR_ofWTFDfdgAYQgi8mR5c76ie8XQ2wjegk7-yyU5zdRVHKybQgUlQ=h36' data-full='https://lh3.googleusercontent.com/p3J-ZSPOLtuBBR_ofWTFDfdgAYQgi8mR5c76ie8XQ2wjegk7-yyU5zdRVHKybQgUlQ=h36' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 06:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536177' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains Poisson disk sampling as a method to generate evenly distributed points without clustering, useful for graphics, spatial sampling, and ML visualization. Includes intuition and practical tips.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍泊松盘采样，生成均匀、无聚簇的点分布，广泛用于图形、空间采样和可视化。给出直观解释与实用建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(178) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://phys.org/news/2026-08-mad-honey-heart-sold-online.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#x27;Mad honey&#x27; that can stop your heart is being sold online</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在线销售的“疯狂蜂蜜”可能致命心脏停跳</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 28, 2026 / 02:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49476239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49476239' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 113</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49476239'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mad honey, produced by certain Rhododendron species, contains toxins that can trigger dizziness, fainting, and potentially fatal heart irregularities. It’s being marketed online despite safety risks, highlighting gaps in online marketplace regulation and consumer protection for natural products.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由特定杜鹃属植物所产的‘疯狂蜂蜜’含有可引发眩晕、昏厥甚至致命性心律不齐的毒素，目前在一些在线平台销售，暴露出在线市场监管和自然产物安全保护的不足。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 15:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49528895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49528895' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Weedout is a Safari extension that hides videos labeled by YouTube&#x27;s AI labeling system. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Weedout 是一款 Safari 扩展，用于隐藏 YouTube 的 AI 标注视频。它展示了在自动标注日益普及的时代，提升用户对内容和元数据控制的思想。此类工具反映出用户对个性化内容控制的日益需求。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49551925' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(187) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://aginggamer.net/game-industry/sony-makes-bold-claim-about-game-ownership/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sony makes bold claim about game ownership</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>索尼就游戏所有权提出大胆主张</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 08:44 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49551925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 179</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49551925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 269</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49551925'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sony asserts stronger ownership rights for digital game licenses, possibly challenging the conventional licensing model around purchased games and resale within its ecosystems. The stance could affect consumer expectations, portability across platforms, and how publishers monetize digital libraries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>索尼就数字游戏许可的所有权提出更强主张，或对传统的购买许可与数字库在跨平台流转上的模式造成挑战。这一态度可能影响玩家的权利期望、跨平台可移植性以及发行商对数字库的盈利模式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://aginggamer.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/PlayStation-Logo_sized.webp' data-full='https://aginggamer.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/PlayStation-Logo_sized.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49503956' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49503956' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://kuleshov-group.github.io/blog/blog/2026/how-to-build-a-diffusion-language-model/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How to build a diffusion language model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何构建扩散式语言模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 16:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49503956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 178</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49503956' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 20</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49503956'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents an approach to building diffusion-based language models, covering data prep, model architecture, training, and sampling. Useful for researchers exploring non-autoregressive generative approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了构建扩散式语言模型的思路，包括数据准备、模型结构、训练和采样等要点，适用于探索非自回归生成方法的研究者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://kuleshov-group.github.io/blog/assets/img/ar-diffusion-comparison.png' data-full='https://kuleshov-group.github.io/blog/assets/img/ar-diffusion-comparison.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://admiralcloudberg.medium.com/a-matter-of-millimeters-the-story-of-qantas-flight-32-bdaa62dc98e7' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Qantas Airbus A380 engine failure in 2010 (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2010 年 Qantas A380 发动机故障（2023 年回顾）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 11:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 177</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540565' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49540565'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This retrospective revisits a 2010 engine failure on a Qantas A380, examining root causes, maintenance practices, and lessons for airline safety and regulatory oversight. It connects historical incidents to modern reliability expectations for large turbofan engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾 2010 年 Qantas A380 的发动机故障，分析根本原因、维护实践与对航空安全及监管的启示。将历史事件与对大型涡扇发动机的可靠性期望联系起来，并强调微小公差与检修制度在大规模运营中的重要性。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 06:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49536114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 59</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49536114'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Security researchers found six CVEs in curl after major AI companies reported zero vulnerabilities. Highlights ongoing need for proactive auditing, patch cycles, and supply-chain security in ubiquitous tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在两家 AI 巨头报告零漏洞后，curl 仍披露六个 CVE，强调对广泛使用工具持续进行主动审计、补丁更新和供应链安全的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tuta.com/blog/android-side-load-apps-google' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>120 days until Google restricts side-loading, declaring war on Android freedom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>距离 Google 限制侧加载还有 120 天，宣称要对 Android 自由开战</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 11:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49554372' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49554372'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google plans to restrict sideloading in 120 days, intensifying control over app distribution and potentially impacting devices and developers that rely on alternative app stores. The move tests the balance between security and user freedom, with broad implications for the Android ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google 计划在 120 天内限制侧载应用，强化对应用分发的控制，可能影响依赖替代应用商店的设备和开发者。此举在安全与用户自由之间拉扯，对 Android 生态系统具有广泛影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(195) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://packagemain.tech/p/gitignore-everything-by-default' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>.gitignore Everything by Default</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>默认忽略一切的 Git 忽略规则</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 06:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49576258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 173</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49576258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 170</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49576258'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A discussion about changing default repository behavior to ignore everything unless explicitly tracked, challenging conventional workflows. The argument emphasizes reducing accidental commits of sensitive or large files, while noting onboarding and collaboration friction. It offers practical templates and policy considerations for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论将默认行为改为新仓库忽略一切，除非显式跟踪，这对传统工作流提出挑战。观点指出这种严格默认有助于减少无意提交敏感或大文件的情况，但也可能增加新成员的上手难度和协作成本，同时提供实用模板与实践要点。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(198) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.master.dev/react-now-rusted-all-the-way-out/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Rust React Compiler is now native in Vite</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 10:49 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49567873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49567873' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vite now ships a native integration with a Rust-based React compiler, enabling faster builds and leaner bundles by leveraging Rust tooling in the frontend pipeline. The change signals growing interest in leveraging Rust for frontend tooling to improve performance and safety.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vite 现已原生集成基于 Rust 的 React 编译器，利用 Rust 工具链提升构建速度并缩小打包体积。此改动传达了在前端工具链中进一步使用 Rust 以提升性能与安全性的趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49580778' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49580778' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(199) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wsj.com/business/diamond-mines-are-closing-and-south-africans-have-few-alternatives-fa3b8cbf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>South African diamond mines are closing due to weak sales and lab-grown stones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>南非钻石矿因销量低迷与实验室培育宝石冲击而关闭</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 14:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 167</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580778'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Due to weak demand and competition from lab-grown stones, South Africa&#x27;s diamond mines are shutting down, with broader implications for jobs and regional economies and a shift in the diamond market.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于需求疲软和实验室培育宝石冲击传统市场，南非钻石矿业正在关闭。这将对就业和地方经济产生深远影响，同时也凸显珠宝行业向替代材料转型的压力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49511917' data-tags='Culture,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49511917' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.heise.de/en/news/Zuse-Computer-Museum-ZCOM-in-Hoyerswerda-faces-closure-11344513.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Konrad Zuse Museum shutting down due to lack of funding</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>孔拉德·祖斯博物馆因资金短缺将关闭</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 09:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 81</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49511917'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Konrad Zuse Museum in Hoyerswerda faces closure after a funding shortfall, risking the loss of a key stop on the history of computation. The situation highlights how small museums struggle to secure stable funding and underscores the need for new fundraising approaches and public support to preserve computing heritage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>霍伊尔斯韦尔达的孔拉德·祖斯博物馆因经费短缺面临关闭，可能让早期计算机史的重要展馆烟消云散。此事暴露出小型博物馆在资金方面的脆弱性，亟需新的筹资渠道与政府及社会力量的支持来保护计算机遗产。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://heise.cloudimg.io/v7/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/1/0/6/6/1/7/000-92e64daee2619092.webp?func=bound&amp;height=1200&amp;org_if_sml=1&amp;q=85&amp;width=1200' data-full='https://heise.cloudimg.io/v7/_www-heise-de_/imgs/18/5/1/0/6/6/1/7/000-92e64daee2619092.webp?func=bound&amp;height=1200&amp;org_if_sml=1&amp;q=85&amp;width=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49521315' data-tags='Legal,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49521315' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(201) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/08/eff-courts-dont-rewrite-copyright-over-ai-hype' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EFF to Courts: Don&#x27;t Rewrite Copyright over AI Hype</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EFF 要求法院不要在 AI 热潮中改写版权法</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 05:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521315' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 188</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49521315'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EFF argues that courts should resist broad rewrites of copyright law prompted by AI hype, warning that sweeping changes could chill innovation and misread fair use and data licensing. The brief calls for careful, case-by-case analysis of training data, attribution, and ownership rather than broad statutory overhauls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EFF 呼吁法院不要被 AI 热潮牵着走，对版权法进行大规模、笼统的改写。应坚持逐案分析训练数据、署名与作品归属等问题，避免因误判而扼杀创新。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ai-robot-warhol-2.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/ai-robot-warhol-2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如何获得免费 .arpa 域名</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 15:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49515558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 163</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49515558' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49515558'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky guide explains how to obtain a free ARPA domain, touching on DNS history and legacy infrastructure. The piece uses the quirk to spark discussion about internet governance and naming systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇古怪的指南讲述如何获得免费 ARPA 域名，涉及 DNS 历史与遗留基础设施。通过这个趣题引发对互联网治理与命名系统的讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49546800' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49546800' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.yahoo.com/news/16-old-girls-win-google-science-fair-simple-194434703.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Three schoolgirls in Kinsale pulled up a pea plant covered in warts (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>肯塞尔三名女学生发现长疣的豌豆苗（2016）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 00:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49546800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 161</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49546800' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49546800'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Three schoolgirls in Kinsale pulled up a pea plant covered in warts and turned the find into a long-running home science project. Over three years they tested about 13,000 seeds in a spare bedroom, with the wart-associated bacteria guiding their barley experiments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>肯塞尔的三名女学生在发现一株长疣的豌豆苗后没放弃，展开了一场持续多年的家庭科学实验。三年间她们在一间空房中试验约1.3万粒种子，豌豆疣中的细菌成为大麦研究的线索。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://scienceblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/pea-plant-roots-nodules.png' data-full='https://scienceblog.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/pea-plant-roots-nodules.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49566193' data-tags='Privacy,Open Source,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49566193' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://desec.io/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>deSEC – Free Secure DNS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>deSEC：免费安全 DNS</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 08:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49566193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49566193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 56</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49566193'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>deSEC offers free, DNSSEC-secured DNS with emphasis on privacy and open-source tooling. The project provides an alternative to centralized DNS services, aiming to reduce surveillance risk while keeping DNS resolvers accessible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>deSEC 提供免费、具 DNSSEC 的安全 DNS，强调隐私与开源工具。该项目为对公有 DNS 的集中化替代方案，力求在降低监控风险的同时保持解析服务的可访问性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://desec.io/assets/logo.svg' data-full='https://desec.io/assets/logo.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49551589' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49551589' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.x.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grok outage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grok 服务中断</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 08:26 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49551589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 159</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49551589' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49551589'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grok, the AI assistant from X (formerly Twitter) faced an outage, disrupting access for users and developers relying on its API. The incident spotlights the risk of single-vendor AI services for workflows and the push for more resilient, multi-provider strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grok 服务遭遇中断，影响了用户和开发者通过 API 访问的能力。这一事件凸显对单一厂商 AI 服务在工作流中的脆弱性，以及推动采用多供应商、冗余方案的重要性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49564714' data-tags='Legal,Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49564714' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://prospect.org/2026/08/21/pentagon-minerals-deal-department-defense-cerberus-capital-alan-waldenberg-stephen-feinberg/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Top Pentagon Official Contracted Personal Lawyer to Handle Minerals Deal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五角大楼高层官员为矿产交易聘请私人律师</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 06:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49564714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49564714' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49564714'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A top Pentagon official hired a personal lawyer to manage a minerals deal connected to the defense supply chain, raising questions about conflicts of interest and ethical oversight. The arrangement underscores how lucrative security-related mineral deals can invite private legal counsel and shadowy risk considerations, prompting scrutiny over how the department handles sensitive vendor relationships.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五角大楼一位高级官员为一项与国防供应链相关的矿产交易聘请私人律师处理事宜，引发对利益冲突与伦理监督的关注。这一安排凸显国防相关矿产交易的潜在高额利益，以及对部委如何管理敏感供应商关系的审查需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/prospect.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Groz-Stephen-Feinberg-DOD-082126.jpg?fit=2000%2C1333&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/prospect.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Groz-Stephen-Feinberg-DOD-082126.jpg?fit=2000%2C1333&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49567310' data-tags='AI,Science,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49567310' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gmcgoldr.github.io/2026/09/04/llm-next-token-predictors.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>“Next-token predictor” is the wrong mental model for LLMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把LLMs当作下一个标记预测器是错误的认知模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 10:09 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49567310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49567310' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 304</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49567310'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that &#x27;next-token predictor&#x27; is an insufficient mental model for LLMs, highlighting emergent reasoning and rich internal representations that go beyond simple token prediction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为把LLMs简化为“下一个标记预测器”是错误的认知模型。需要关注模型的内部表示、推理能力及涌现行为等超出单纯预测下一词的特性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49520896' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49520896' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anildash.com/2025/09/09/how-tim-cook-sold-out-steve-jobs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tim Cook Sold Out Steve Jobs (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蒂姆·库克在 2025 年背离了乔布斯精神</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49520896' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49520896'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tim Cook’s 2025 Apple is criticized for diverging from Steve Jobs’ vision, with arguments that the leadership shift emphasizes monetization and growth over design purity and innovation. The piece suggests such changes risk diluting Apple’s brand ethos even as they extend the company’s scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇观点文章认为蒂姆·库克对苹果的定位偏离了乔布斯时代的设计理念与创新精神，转而更注重营收和规模扩张。作者担心此类转变可能削弱苹果的品牌基因和创新优势，但也承认增长带来的市场影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.anildash.com/images/cook-trump-gift.jpeg' data-full='https://www.anildash.com/images/cook-trump-gift.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工分析智能指数v4.2汇总了AI在各领域的进展，给出能力、安全性与落地等指标。结果对政策制定、研究方向和行业投资具有参考意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ask HN: Who is using MCP in production?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ask HN：谁在生产环境中使用 MCP？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 04:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548600' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49548256' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/books/2026/sep/03/gloria-steinem-groundbreaking-feminist-campaigner-dies-aged-92' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gloria Steinem has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>格洛丽亚·斯坦姆去世</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 03:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49548256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49548256'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Gloria Steinem, a pioneering feminist, author, and organizer, has died at 92. Her career helped shape gender equality in media, tech, and society, leaving a blueprint for advocacy and intersectional activism.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>格洛丽亚·斯坦姆，一位开创性女权主义者、作家与组织者，去世，享年 92 岁。她的事业深刻影响了媒体、科技与社会的性别平等，留下了倡导与交叉性行动的宝贵范本。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/40b70b8f5b6aa7ba38af77c2ac99e035c0c765db/0_68_5256_3154/master/5256.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=43971682c6296560810fc844e5b7bef8' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/40b70b8f5b6aa7ba38af77c2ac99e035c0c765db/0_68_5256_3154/master/5256.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=43971682c6296560810fc844e5b7bef8' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49512856' data-tags='AI,Career'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49512856' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://muratbuffalo.blogspot.com/2026/08/the-safest-job-from-ai-may-be-writing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The safest job from AI may be writing</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>从 AI 来看最安全的工作可能是写作</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 11:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49512856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49512856' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 206</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Career'>Career</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49512856'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues writing may be relatively resilient to automation because nuanced tone, audience alignment, and editorial judgment remain hard for AI to master. It suggests writers embrace AI as a tool to augment their craft rather than compete with it.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为写作在自动化浪潮中相对更具韧性，因为文风细腻、面向读者的对齐以及编辑判断等能力仍然难以被 AI 完全取代。建议作者将 AI 视作提升能力的工具，而非对手。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RotaryCell 记录如何用 ESP32-S3 将未改动的旋转拨号电话接入 LTE，连接传统电话与现代蜂窝网络。此项目凸显了对遗留设备重新利用的技术挑战与机遇。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Astra&#x27;s recurrent architecture draws attention to potential trade-offs between speed, memory, and stability in long-horizon prompts. <div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49527232' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(220) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomverbeure.github.io/2026/08/23/Tektronix-TDS7104-Refurbishing.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Refurbishing a Tektronix TDS7104 Oscilloscope</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>翻新 Tektronix TDS7104 示波器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 12:55 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527232' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49527232'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on refurbishing project revisits the Tektronix TDS7104 oscilloscope, covering teardown, calibration, and parts sourcing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇动手实操文章记录了对经典 Tektronix TDS7104 示波器的翻新过程，包括拆解、校准以及零件来源。文中点出常见故障点与翻新中的挑战，以及爱好者为何仍能从修复老式仪器中受益。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 19:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49582656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49582656' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 63</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49582656'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ben Evans argues that AI-enabled tools will transform work by changing how teams adopt and orchestrate capabilities, not merely by model power. The piece outlines a framework for tool-driven transformation and strategic organizational bets.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者分析了 AI 驱动工具如何重塑工作方式、生产力和商业模式，认为真正的影响来自团队对工具的采纳与协同，而不仅是模型能力。提出关于工具驱动变革的思考框架，以及企业应当做出的关键取舍。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 05:12 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49497996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49497996' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 145</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49497996'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As language models become dominant, many languages lose digital presence and resources, shrinking linguistic diversity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>随着大型语言模型的普及，许多语言在数字世界的存在与资源减少，语言多样性正趋于缩小。文章总结了相关证据并呼吁加强政策与研究支持以保护小语种。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49543007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49543007' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 116</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49543007'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber abruptly exits Nigeria and Uganda, citing strategic or regulatory reasons. The move affects riders and drivers who rely on ride-hailing, potentially accelerating local competition and prompting regulatory debates about platform gig work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Uber 立即关闭在尼日利亚和乌干达的运营，原因涉及战略或监管因素。此举影响使用打车服务的用户与司机，或促使当地竞争加剧并引发关于平台型零工经济的监管讨论。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49558086' data-tags='Health'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49558086' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(229) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/ozempic-and-other-glp-1s-are-being-linked-to-fewer-serious-infections-including-tb-2000806796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLP-1s are being linked to fewer serious infections, including TB</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLP-1 药物被关联于减少严重感染（包括结核）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 15:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49558086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49558086' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 82</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49558086'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Observational data suggest GLP-1 medications may be associated with a lower risk of serious infections, including TB. While not proving causation, the finding spurs interest in potential immune-modulating effects and calls for focused clinical studies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>观察性数据表明 GLP-1 药物可能与严重感染风险下降相关，包括结核病。虽无法证明因果关系，但这一发现促使研究其潜在的免疫调节作用，并需要进一步的临床研究来证实。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49577985' data-tags='Health,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49577985' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(230) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/news/how-tobacco-industry-drove-rise-ultra-processed-foods' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How the Tobacco Industry Drove the Rise of Ultra-Processed Foods (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>烟草行业推动超加工食品崛起的幕后</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 09:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49577985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49577985' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49577985'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The tobacco industry helped normalize ultra-processed foods by marketing, lobbying, and shaping consumer tastes, contributing to public health challenges; this ties into discussions of industry influence on nutrition policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>烟草行业通过市场营销、游说和影响消费口味，推动了超加工食品的兴起，进而带来公共卫生挑战。这揭示了行业影响对营养政策的作用。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/themes/custom/vcr/assets/images/ucb-logo-blue.svg' data-full='https://vcresearch.berkeley.edu/themes/custom/vcr/assets/images/ucb-logo-blue.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49501791' data-tags='Politics,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49501791' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(231) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://economist.com/by-invitation/2026/08/30/the-world-may-have-less-time-than-it-thinks-on-climate-change' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The world may have less time than it thinks on climate change</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>气候变化的紧迫时间可能比想象的更短</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 12:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49501791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 138</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49501791' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49501791'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that the window to avert dangerous climate change may be narrower than many expect, driven by accelerating feedbacks and higher emissions than anticipated. It outlines implications for policy, technology, and finance in meeting climate goals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为避免危险变暖的时间窗可能比许多人预期的更窄，受加速的反馈机制和高于预期的排放推动。它讨论在实现气候目标方面的政策、技术与资金方面的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on RuneScape’s 2004 era shows how the game delivered multiplayer experiences over dial-up using lean data and clever networking. It offers design lessons for building scalable online games under bandwidth constraints.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(234) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.righto.com/2026/08/spacelab-core-memory.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cores in space: The core memory module from a 1980 Spacelab computer</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49502214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49502214' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49502214'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores a magnetic core memory module recovered from a 1980 Spacelab computer, illustrating how early memory technology worked and why it mattered for aerospace reliability. The piece connects vintage hardware to contemporary memory concepts, highlighting lessons in durability and engineering trade-offs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述从 1980 年 Spacelab 计算机中回收的核心内存模块，展示早期内存技术的工作原理及其在航天领域的可靠性作用。文章将老派硬件与现代内存概念联系起来，强调耐用性和工程取舍的经验。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(235) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sander.ai/2026/08/24/continuous-dlms.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Continuous Diffusion Language Models (CDLM&#x27;s)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>连续扩散语言模型（CDLM）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 13:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49502611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 132</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49502611' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49502611'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Outlines the idea of Continuous Diffusion Language Models, proposing a model that blends diffusion-based generation with language tasks for improved controllability and editability. It discusses potential architectures, challenges, and how CDLMs could complement traditional transformers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍连续扩散语言模型的概念，提出将扩散式生成与语言任务结合，以提升可控性和可编辑性。讨论潜在的架构、挑战，以及 CDLMs 如何与传统 Transformer 互补。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ryangreenberg.com/til/git-branches-by-commit-date/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Sort branches by last commit date</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>按最后提交日期对分支排序</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 25, 2026 / 07:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49435285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 131</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49435285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49435285'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A practical Git tip for repository hygiene: sorting branches by their most recent commit date helps identify stale branches and clean up workflows. It can save time on large repos and reveal active areas of work for teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个实用的 Git 技巧，按最近一次提交日期对分支进行排序，能帮助识别已过时的分支并优化工作流。对于大型仓库尤其有用，可以节省时间并凸显活跃的开发区域。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(242) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.netbsd.org/tnf/entry/netbsd_9_5_released_and' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NetBSD 9.5 released and EOL for NetBSD-9</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NetBSD 9.5 发布，NetBSD-9 进入终止支持</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49587636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 124</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49587636' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49587636'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 9.5 arrives with security fixes and improvements, while NetBSD-9 reaches end-of-life. Users should plan migration to newer releases to stay supported and secure. The release underscores NetBSD&#x27;s ongoing commitment to portability and reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NetBSD 9.5 发布，NetBSD-9 系列进入停止维护阶段。用户应规划升级到更新版本以获得持续支持和安全性。此举彰显 NetBSD 在可移植性与稳定性方面的持续承诺。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical look at static allocation and constant work, arguing that fixed resource planning can yield predictable latency and simpler reasoning for systems programmers. Practical guidance helps choose when preallocation pays off.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>METR 对 OpenAI / Hugging Face 黑客事件的调查报告</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 16:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49543841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 122</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49543841' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49543841'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>METR&#x27;s report synthesizes findings from an OpenAI and Hugging Face security incident, highlighting how attackers exploited weaknesses in model sharing ecosystems. Key takeaways include the need for stronger collaboration controls, governance, and patch responsiveness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>METR 的报告汇总了 OpenAI 与 Hugging Face 安全事件的调查结果，指出攻击者如何利用模型分享生态中的漏洞。核心结论包括加强协作治理、改进漏洞响应和增强管控。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(250) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dpreview.com/news/the-colorchecker-photographys-most-important-24-squares-turns-50/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The ColorChecker, photography&#x27;s most important 24 squares, turns 50</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ColorChecker：摄影最重要的24格色卡迎来50周年</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 15:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49529398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 120</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49529398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49529398'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The ColorChecker 24-square card marks its 50th anniversary as a foundational calibration standard for accurate color across lighting. Its enduring utility persists even as digital tools evolve, shaping how photographers approach color management.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ColorChecker 的50周年凸显其作为稳定色彩校准标准的长期价值，在不同光照条件下帮助保持肤色与色彩的一致性。尽管有更新的工具，24格色卡仍是摄影工作流的基础，影响工作室和相机的色彩判断。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early Scratch exposure correlates with lasting interest in programming for some people, opening doors to STEM careers, but outcomes vary widely. The discussion highlights how entry-level, beginner-friendly tools can shape long-term technical trajectories, and considerations for educators and researchers.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A spacetime database project faces questions about scaling as workloads grow, with analyses comparing throughput, latency, and resource needs under realistic usage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>关于时空数据库的扩展性问题在工作负载增长时引发关注，分析比较吞吐、延迟和在现实使用场景下的资源需求。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49559522' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49559522' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(254) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/xanadu' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project Xanadu: Even More Hindsight (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xanadu 项目：更深的回顾</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 18:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49559522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 118</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49559522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49559522'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece revisits Xanadu&#x27;s core ideas—transclusion, bidirectional links, and robust versioning—and explains why they were ahead of their time, yet impractical to deploy at scale. It also draws lessons for today&#x27;s web, such as provenance, offline-first architecture, and data integrity against link rot.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回溯 Xanadu 的核心理念——可转引/跨引用、双向链接和强版本控制——并解释为何这些想法虽领先时代、却难以在大规模落地。它还从中提炼出对当今网络的启示，如溯源、离线优先的架构，以及防止链接失效的数据完整性。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(255) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lttlabs.com/articles/2026/09/02/delidded-intel-i9-14900ks' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Delidded Intel I9-14900KS CT Scan</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A CT scan of a delidded Intel i9-14900KS reveals the internal stack, die, and solder layer layout, offering a rare peek at the CPU’s packaging. The teardown provides insights into manufacturing choices and potential cooling or overclocking implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对解封后的英特尔 i9-14900KS 进行 CT 扫描，披露了内部堆叠、晶体和焊料层的分布，提供了对 CPU 封装的罕见洞见。该拆解能帮助理解制造工艺的取舍及对散热与超频潜在影响。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(257) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/penlume/status/2095372672356212876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RSA-260 Factorized</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RSA-260 的因子分解</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 22:35 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49546284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 116</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49546284' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49546284'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RSA-260’s factorization marks another milestone in practical factoring of large semi-primes using GNFS. The result informs cryptographic parameter choices and reinforces the push toward larger keys or quantum-resistant schemes to protect long-term data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RSA-260 的因子分解标志着大型半素数可因分解的又一里程碑，使用 GNFS 等现代算法实现。结果影响密码参数的选择，进一步推动对更大密钥或量子抗性方案的需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1911652961379401728/Mf-cc4Uw_200x200.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1911652961379401728/Mf-cc4Uw_200x200.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49521623' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(259) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frn.sh/io-uring/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Io_uring Without Readahead</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Io_uring：不使用 Readahead</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 06:19 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521623' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49521623'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece argues for running io_uring without readahead in certain workloads to reduce latency and memory pressure. It discusses when readahead helps versus when it hurts and offers practical tuning tips for Linux I/O paths. A solid read for systems engineers optimizing storage-heavy apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中提出在特定工作负载下可让 io_uring 跳过预读取以降低延迟和内存压力。讨论了何时预读取有帮助、何时会带来负担，并给出 Linux I/O 路径的实用调优建议。对优化存储密集型应用的系统工程师很有价值。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 28, 2026 / 22:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49487099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 112</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49487099' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49487099'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An incident at MIT involving exposure to dimethyl mercury underscores the extreme toxicity of organomercury compounds and the long-term risks in labs. The story highlights lab safety reforms and the importance of rigorous chemical handling protocols.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MIT 的二甲基汞暴露事件凸显了有机汞化合物的极端毒性与潜在长期影响。事件推动了实验室安全改革、应急处置以及化学品操作规范的强化。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49499095' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(264) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ziglang.org/devlog/2026/#2026-08-27' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Zig: Pointer Stability for ArrayLists</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Zig：ArrayLists 的指针稳定性</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 07:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 111</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49499095' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49499095'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Zig devlog details improvements to ArrayList pointer stability, enabling safer growth without invalidating references and improving performance guarantees for systems code. This work helps Zig approach memory-safety goals while staying close to low-level control.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开发日志介绍了 Zig 在 ArrayList 指针稳定性方面的改进，确保在扩容时不失效引用并提升性能保证。该工作有助于 Zig 在追求内存安全的同时保持对底层控制的灵活性。</p>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49534936' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49534936' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(267) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='http://muratbuffalo.blogspot.com/2026/05/the-two-abstractions-of-system-design.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Two Abstractions of System Design: Hide or Reduce</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>系统设计的两种抽象：隐藏与简化</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 04:54 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49534936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49534936' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49534936'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two guiding abstractions shape system design: hide complexity to provide simple interfaces, or reduce operations to improve efficiency. Each approach trades off flexibility, performance, and maintainability. The article argues for balancing these abstractions to manage large-scale systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>系统设计受两种抽象指引：隐藏复杂性以提供简洁接口，或通过简化操作以提升效率。每种方法都在灵活性、性能和可维护性之间做出权衡。文章主张在大规模系统中平衡这两种抽象以实现可持续设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEgUV8paJsfBDCdaizCxyZLf_tgGZc_L1c6HYhST-bupV3snzItwcj-3TzIJnc20dDeTmoFeMaEHxyZpxrqsgI80le-78m0BN7QqcMGPBkhwTLTu-BaWTc0DQ9rpXsag-tonr1n-TYZXz2sRYCk7HQaB94VGFboZ8sPvIL9_mIPGhBpOqL3B_42ueVNyq9o=w393-h400' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/a/AVvXsEgUV8paJsfBDCdaizCxyZLf_tgGZc_L1c6HYhST-bupV3snzItwcj-3TzIJnc20dDeTmoFeMaEHxyZpxrqsgI80le-78m0BN7QqcMGPBkhwTLTu-BaWTc0DQ9rpXsag-tonr1n-TYZXz2sRYCk7HQaB94VGFboZ8sPvIL9_mIPGhBpOqL3B_42ueVNyq9o=w393-h400' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49530807' data-tags='Gaming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49530807' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(268) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/lite/story/9.7328614' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>New type of dice guarantees no tie when deciding who goes first</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新型骰子确保先手决议不打平</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 18:50 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49530807' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49530807' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 92</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49530807'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new dice design promises unique outcomes without ties when choosing who goes first, potentially changing how casual games and competitive setups handle randomness. The design uses weighted faces or clever geometry to ensure a decisive result.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种新型骰子设计承诺在决定先手时避免平局，可能改变休闲游戏和竞赛中对随机性的处理。设计通过加权面或巧妙的几何结构确保结果定夺。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49508059' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49508059' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(269) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.mdubakov.me/malleable-software-solid-bases-custom-code/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malleable software = solid bases and custom code</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可塑软件：稳固基础与定制代码</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 03:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49508059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49508059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49508059'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Malleable software argues for a strong, stable core with extensible layers that can be customized without touching the base code. The approach stresses clean interfaces and modular design to support rapid experimentation while maintaining reliability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>可塑软件强调以稳定的核心为基础，辅以可扩展的定制层，便于团队在不改动底层的情况下灵活定制功能。其方法论强调清晰的接口、强抽象和职责分离，以在提高研发效率的同时保持系统可靠性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://storage.ghost.io/c/c4/c6/c4c61cc9-7edd-4a33-857b-26f10fd22b0b/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/malleable-software--1-.png' data-full='https://storage.ghost.io/c/c4/c6/c4c61cc9-7edd-4a33-857b-26f10fd22b0b/content/images/size/w1200/2026/08/malleable-software--1-.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49463397' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(270) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/electric-rain-can-eat-through-metal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electric rain can eat through metal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>电雨能侵蚀金属</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 27, 2026 / 05:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49463397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 108</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49463397' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49463397'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Electric rain describes a phenomenon where electrically charged rain droplets or arcing environments accelerate corrosion of metals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>电雨现象指带电雨滴或放电环境加速金属腐蚀。文章探讨这一现象对基础设施、航空航天和电子设备的影响，以及在材料与涂层选型上可能需要的改进。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/a3ccc794-1a6f-4785-b66f-648e3ef9f918/Raindrops-3000.jpg?m=1787762736.102&amp;w=1200' data-full='https://static.scientificamerican.com/dam/asset/a3ccc794-1a6f-4785-b66f-648e3ef9f918/Raindrops-3000.jpg?m=1787762736.102&amp;w=1200' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(271) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vermaden.wordpress.com/2026/09/06/amd-based-freebsd-desktop-reloaded/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AMD Based FreeBSD Desktop Reloaded</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49582719'>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49515662' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cgicoffee.com/blog/2026/04/tcl-tk-develop-cross-platform-cli-gui-tools-tutorial-guide' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Develop Cross-Platform CLI and GUI Tools with Tcl/Tk</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用 Tcl/Tk 开发跨平台的 CLI 与 GUI 工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 15:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49515662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49515662' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 55</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49515662'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tutorial explains using Tcl/Tk to build cross-platform CLI and GUI tools, covering setup, packaging, and practical examples. Tcl/Tk remains a lightweight, portable choice for small tooling and internal utilities, though it trails modern ecosystems in updates and community momentum.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>教程讲解如何使用 Tcl/Tk 构建跨平台的 CLI 与 GUI 工具，涵盖环境搭建、打包与实用示例。Tcl/Tk 仍是小型工具和内部工具的轻量且可移植选项，但在更新频率与生态活力方面落后于更现代的方案。</p>
