</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49591672' data-tags='Security,Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49591672' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(273) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/Liquid_BTC/status/2096696272447218108' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hackers have withdrawn ~4k BTC (~$320M) from the Liquid Federation wallet</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>黑客已从 Liquid Federation 钱包提走约 4k BTC</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 15:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49591672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49591672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 57</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49591672'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hackers have withdrawn about 4,000 BTC (roughly $320 million) from the Liquid Federation wallet, underscoring persistent on-chain security risks. The move highlights ongoing concerns about custodial controls and real-time monitoring of treasury wallets. It reinforces the need for robust multi-sig and incident response practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>黑客已从 Liquid Federation 钱包提走约 4,000 BTC，约合 3.2 亿美元，凸显链上安全风险的持续存在。此举再次暴露托管控制与资金监控的隐忧，强调需要更强的多签及事件响应机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/card_img/2094942939721920512/sLH5zf5k?format=jpg&amp;name=orig' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/card_img/2094942939721920512/sLH5zf5k?format=jpg&amp;name=orig' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49522367' data-tags='Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49522367' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(274) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://idpro.org/authorization-terminology-is-a-mess-lets-fix-it/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Authorization terminology is a mess: Let&#x27;s fix it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>授权术语混乱：让我们统一口径</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 07:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49522367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 105</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49522367' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49522367'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AuthN vs AuthZ, permissions, entitlements, scopes. The piece argues for standardizing terminology across identity and access management, proposing precise definitions and a shared vocabulary to reduce confusion for developers and operators.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该文呼吁统一授权相关术语，厘清身份验证与授权、权限、权限范围、授权等概念的差异。通过提出清晰定义和共用词汇，帮助开发者与运维人员减少混淆、提升协作效率。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://idpro.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image.png' data-full='https://idpro.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HN Match Maker 是一个将求职贴『Who Wants to Be Hired?』与招聘贴『Who&#x27;s Hiring?』进行匹配的工具，利用社群信息促进招聘与求职的对接。它体现了社区驱动的招聘工具能降低信息检索成本。此类本地化协作工具在技术社群中具备一定吸引力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Magic Eye tube is a neon indicator used in vintage gear to visualize signal strength. It operates by the phosphor-lit window bending with amplitude, offering a nostalgic look at pre-digital level meters.</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The car industry conducted an A/B test on selling the same car with and without CarPlay, and the results show CarPlay availability meaningfully shifts buyer preferences. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>汽车行业对同一车型是否配备 CarPlay 进行了 A/B 测试，结果显示 CarPlay 的可用性显著影响了买家偏好。这一发现体现了嵌入式生态系统在产品设计、定价与购车选择中的作用，也凸显了软件集成对硬件产品的商业影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鱼坏糖好，以及其他中世纪饮食观念</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 12:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49513721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49513721' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 75</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49513721'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This examination of medieval dietary beliefs shows how ideas about what constitutes healthy eating were shaped by religion, season, and status rather than science. It contrasts with modern nutrition, illustrating how culture and lore influenced what people ate and avoided.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文梳理中世纪的饮食观念，揭示关于何谓健康饮食的看法往往由宗教、季节与社会地位等因素塑造，而非科学证据。与现代营养观形成对照，史事揭示了文化与传说如何左右人们的饮食选择。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(296) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nesbitt.io/2026/09/01/git-submodules-as-a-package-manager.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git Submodules as a Package Manager</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>把 Git 子模组当成包管理器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 02:36 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49519850'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take suggests using Git submodules as a lightweight package manager, enabling exact, versioned dependencies with repeatable checkouts. The approach trades off simplicity for complexity in workflows, with caveats around nested repos, updates, and toolchain gaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一种把 Git 子模组当作轻量包管理器的做法，能够实现版本化依赖和可重复的检出。该思路在简化与复杂性之间取舍，需权衡嵌套仓库、递归更新与现有工具链的局限。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://research.uga.edu/news/blue-light-has-a-surprising-effect-on-your-eyes-study-finds/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Study: Blue light impairs the eye&#x27;s ability to distinguish fine detail most</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>蓝光会削弱眼睛分辨细节能力的研究</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 01:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49507121'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blue light impairs the eye&#x27;s ability to distinguish fine detail more than commonly assumed, with practical implications for display design and eye health. It suggests practical steps to reduce exposure, especially during late hours or precision tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现蓝光对辨别细节能力的影响超出常规认知，给屏幕设计和眼部健康带来启示。研究建议在晚间和需要高细节判断的场景降低蓝光暴露。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://research.uga.edu/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/19/2026/08/yaw-buabeng-1040x600-1.jpg' data-full='https://research.uga.edu/news/wp-content/uploads/sites/19/2026/08/yaw-buabeng-1040x600-1.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49590738' data-tags='Show HN,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49590738' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(299) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/marsbos/mador' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Mador – Make any DOM reactive with a tiny 80-line Proxy state tuple</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Mador——用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组让任意 DOM 实现响应式</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 13:45 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 85</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590738' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49590738'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Mador is a tiny library that makes any DOM reactive using an 80-line Proxy-based state tuple. It demonstrates that practical reactivity can be achieved with a tiny footprint, offering a minimal entry point into how reactive systems work. A useful reference for experimenting with lean, framework-free UI design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Mador 是一个极简库，使用 80 行 Proxy 状态元组即可让任意 DOM 具备响应式能力。它展示了以极小的体积实现响应式的可能性，为理解响应式编程提供了简练的入门范例。对于探索无框架、精简 UI 设计的开发者颇具参考价值。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 83</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527967' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49527967'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A batteryless LED on a PCB business card is powered by the proximity/inductive energy from a phone, enabling a light-up effect without a battery. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一张带无电池 LED 的 PCB 名片通过手机触碰或靠近时产生的能量点亮，展示了能量收集在网络社交中的应用。该设计强调低功耗、易于实现的能量传输方案及成本与耐用性的权衡。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ig Nobel Prize winners showcase humorous yet insightful research across disciplines, often delivering surprisingly practical implications. The list highlights how curiosity and unconventional methods can reveal valuable, real-world insights while entertaining the public. It underscores science’s broader appeal when it blends whimsy with rigor.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Ig 诺贝尔奖得主展示了跨学科的幽默而富有洞察力的研究，通常也带来意想不到的实际意义。这些获奖项目体现了好奇心与另类方法如何在娱乐公众的同时带来有价值的应用。揭示了科学在结合趣味与严谨时的更广泛吸引力。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 81</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49561975' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 64</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Daily carbon-intensity-based pricing is tracked across 38 grids, providing a granular view of how green the power mix is at any moment. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>跨38个电网每天对碳强度进行定价的做法正在被测量与记录，提供电力碳强度对价格影响的细粒度视图。该数据使买卖双方和市场设计者能够以碳信号定价，并鼓励在低碳时段用电，从而影响需求响应和政策制定。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49505257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49505257' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 61</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49505257'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A technical exploration of intrusive linked lists, where list pointers are embedded in the containing objects themselves, trading safety for performance and control. It covers use cases, memory implications, and common pitfalls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对侵入式链表的技术探讨——将链表指针嵌入包含对象中，换取性能与控制权，代价是安全性。涵盖应用场景、内存影响与常见坑。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 08:18 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537632' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 79</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537632' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 38</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A literary critique reframes Poe&#x27;s horror through the lens of confession and intimate truth rather than gore. By focusing on unreliable narrators and buried self-revelations, the piece reveals psychology as the engine of dread.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇文学评论将坡（Poe）恐怖的核心放在人物自白与隐秘的真实之上，而非喧嚣血腥。通过聚焦不可靠叙述者与埋藏的自我揭示，揭示了心理因素如何成为恐怖的驱动力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(317) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.agentconnect.md/blog/grep-beat-lsp-harness/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Grep beats LSP? Why coding agents ignore your fancier tools</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Grep 取胜 LSP？为何编码代理忽略你更花哨的工具</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 20:40 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49560260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49560260' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49560260'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take argues that grep-like search outperforms heavy LSP tooling in coding agents, due to speed and reliability. It outlines why agents default to fast offline indexing and how to design better tooling that complements semantic intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者认为在编码代理中，grep 这类搜索工具往往比大量的 LSP 工具更快更可靠。文中解释原因，并提出让代理工具结合离线索引与语义能力的设计方向。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bnb.im/posts/atproto-essential-resources/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Getting Started with AT Protocol</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AT Protocol 入门指南</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 12:48 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 77</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Getting started with AT Protocol guides developers through onboarding, core docs, and example applications for decentralized social networks. The resources help teams prototype, test, and iterate on decentralized identities and feeds at scale.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一百万只猎鹰失踪：它们是如何被找回的</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 11:19 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>National Geographic recounts a mass falcon disappearance and the subsequent recovery using satellite telemetry, field surveys, and community reporting. The effort highlights how data-driven monitoring and citizen science can aid large-scale wildlife conservation. It underscores the role of technology in tracking and protecting migratory species.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>国家地理讲述了一次大规模猎鹰失踪及随后通过卫星跟踪、实地调查和社区线索找回猎鹰的过程。该行动凸显数据驱动的监测与公民科学在大规模野生动物保护中的作用，并强调科技在追踪与保护候鸟方面的价值。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49530169'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scott Aaronson dives into self-referentiality in large language models, examining how models handle self-referential prompts, recursion, and the illusion of self-awareness, with implications for reasoning, reliability, and AI alignment.</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49580329' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49580329' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(328) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/2014/02/04/finite-time-blowup-for-an-averaged-three-dimensional-navier-stokes-equation/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Finite time blowup for an averaged three-dimensional Navier-Stokes equation (2014)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>三维 Navier–Stokes 平均化方程的有限时间爆破（2014）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 13:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580329' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580329'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tao discusses a finite-time blowup for an averaged 3D Navier–Stokes equation, illustrating how certain averaged models can develop singularities. The result provides a concrete example informing the ongoing discourse on regularity and turbulence modeling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tao 讨论了一种“平均化”三维 Navier–Stokes 方程在有限时间内出现奇点的情况，演示了某些简化模型也可能产生奇点。这一结果为正则性与湍流建模的讨论提供了具体案例。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/02/circuit-1.png' data-full='https://terrytao.wordpress.com/wp-content/uploads/2014/02/circuit-1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(329) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.uber.com/us/en/newsroom/simplerfasteruber/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uber is laying off 10% of staff</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Uber 将裁员 10%</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 05:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49535246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 70</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49535246' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49535246'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Uber announces 10% layoffs amid efficiency drive; may impact teams including AI/ML and data science groups, signaling broader tech sector cost-cutting.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Uber 宣布裁员 10%，以提高效率，可能影响 AI/ML 与数据科学等团队，反映科技行业的成本削减趋势。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(330) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://practical.engineering/blog/2026/9/1/stopping-the-unstoppable' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Stopping the Unstoppable: When an unstoppable force meets a dashpot snubber</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>阻止不可阻挡力：当不可阻挡的力量遇到阻尼器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 10:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49525188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49525188' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49525188'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dashpot snubbers can tame forces that seem unstoppable, with examples from mechanical design and control systems. The article explains damping principles, the interplay of inertia and stiffness, and how feedback helps prevent wear and resonance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>dashpot 阻尼器能抑制看似不可阻挡的力，结合机械设计与控制系统的示例。文中阐释阻尼原理、惯性与刚度的配合，以及如何通过反馈避免磨损与共振。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49488224'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article explains how coaster brakes engage when the rider pedals backward, using a pawl-and-ratchet mechanism to apply braking force. It also covers maintenance tips and common issues.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(333) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thenewatlantis.com/publications/you-probably-own-this-7-eleven' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>You probably own this 7-Eleven (and that&#x27;s why it looks so sad)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你很可能拥有这家 7-Eleven，因此它看起来如此落寞</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 09:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 68</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49511720' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49511720'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An essay arguing that many 7-Eleven stores are franchises owned by local operators who struggle with thin margins, explaining why the storefronts often appear lackluster. It critiques corporate ownership models and their impact on urban retail.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章指出许多 7-Eleven 店由地方业主特许经营，利润微薄，导致门店陈设普遍平庸甚至显得失落。批判大型连锁企业的所有权模式及其对城市零售的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thenewatlantis.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Lawler-share-card.jpg' data-full='https://www.thenewatlantis.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/06/Lawler-share-card.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49590595' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49590595' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(334) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.12540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Harnessing the Universal Geometry of Embeddings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>利用嵌入的普遍几何结构</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 13:31 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49590595' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49590595'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The paper argues for a universal geometry of embeddings, providing a common lens to compare semantic spaces across models. It outlines how geometric structure can inform alignment, transfer learning, and evaluation of embeddings.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出将嵌入向量的几何结构放在统一框架下理解，帮助比较不同模型中的语义空间。该视角有助于改进对齐、迁移学习与嵌入评估的方法。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(335) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quenq.com/apps/vice-city-online/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Play GTA Vice City in the Browser</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 00:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49584123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49584123'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A browser-based version of GTA Vice City lets you play the classic open-world game without any downloads. Built using modern browser tech, it demonstrates how far WebAssembly and related tooling have come in letting heavy games run in-page with minimal friction.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GTA Vice City 现可直接在浏览器中畅玩，无需下载安装。借助现代浏览器技术，这一实现展示了 WebAssembly 等工具在让大型游戏无缝运行于网页中的进展。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49566788' data-tags='AI,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49566788' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(337) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/ai-and-ml/github-copilot/project-hydrafusion-frontier-quality-via-multi-model-orchestration/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Project HydraFusion: Frontier quality via multi-model orchestration</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>HydraFusion 项目：通过多模型编排实现前沿质量</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 09:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49566788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49566788' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49566788'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HydraFusion weaves together multiple models through a unified orchestration layer to boost output quality. By routing tasks to the model best suited for each subproblem, it aims to reach frontier-quality results at scale. This approach could reshape how teams build AI copilots and complex AI pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HydraFusion 将多模型通过统一编排层整合，提升输出质量。通过为不同子任务分配最合适的模型，它旨在实现规模化的前沿级别结果。这一做法可能改变团队构建 AI 辅助工具和复杂 AI 流水线的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/OptA_UI.jpg' data-full='https://github.blog/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/OptA_UI.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 01:57 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49507280' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49507280'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A first-hand take on the OpenStreetMap community gathering, highlighting talks on open data, mapping ethics, and community-driven projects. It underscores the importance of open sources for global mapping and local empowerment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 OpenStreetMap 社区大会 State of the Map 的现场报道，聚焦开放数据、地图伦理与社区驱动项目。强调开源数据在全球地图绘制与地方赋权中的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49586902' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(339) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/scastiel/kado' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Kadō – open-source habit tracker, with non-binary habit score, for iOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kadō：一款开源的非二元习惯评分追踪器（iOS）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 07:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 66</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586902' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49586902'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kadō is an open-source iOS habit tracker that uses a non-binary habit score rather than a simple done/not-done metric. The project highlights inclusive design and invites community contributions to improve its scoring model and features. It showcases how nuanced metrics can better reflect progress.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kadō 是一款开源的 iOS 习惯追踪应用，采用非二元化的习惯评分而非简单完成/未完成的二元指标。该项目强调包容性设计并鼓励社区贡献，以改进评分模型与功能，展示了更细致的进展衡量方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8047ce34c90629a31cc4981c6180825f4c850014bdd205a9111d50e4eadf9f30/scastiel/kado' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/8047ce34c90629a31cc4981c6180825f4c850014bdd205a9111d50e4eadf9f30/scastiel/kado' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/smart-news/when-fruit-is-scarce-these-monkeys-hunt-animals-the-behavior-may-offer-clues-about-the-origins-of-humans-meat-eating-habits-180989412/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When fruit is scarce, these monkeys hunt animals</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49489274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49489274' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers observed certain monkey species switch to meat-eating when fruit is scarce, offering clues about the origins of meat consumption in humans and the flexibility of primate foraging strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者发现部分猴子在果实短缺时转向捕猎动物，提供了人类肉食起源与灵活的灵长类取食策略的线索。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Greg Egan&#x27;s Steffen&#x27;s Polyhedron centers on a mysterious geometric object whose properties push the boundaries of physics and perception. The story uses the polyhedron to probe certainty, knowledge, and the nature of reality.</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49581240' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(345) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/okf-memory/okf-agent-memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OKF Agent Memory – Git-native persistent memory for AI coding agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OKF Agent Memory：面向 AI 编码代理的 Git 原生持久内存</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 15:15 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49581240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49581240' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49581240'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OKF Agent Memory provides git-native persistent memory for AI coding agents, allowing state to persist across sessions and improving reproducibility. The approach integrates versioned state management with agent workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OKF Agent Memory 为 AI 编码代理提供 Git 原生的持久内存，使状态能在跨会话间持久化，提升可重复性和调试效率。该方案将版本化状态管理与代理工作流结合起来。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/99a0c242e318891d2d41238b934937951e2b70e1648557872adc759e0e5b20f2/okf-memory/okf-agent-memory' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/99a0c242e318891d2d41238b934937951e2b70e1648557872adc759e0e5b20f2/okf-memory/okf-agent-memory' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49542123' data-tags='Programming,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49542123' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(346) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.vidact.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vidact – a compiler that turns React into direct DOM operations</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Vidact——将 React 转换为直接 DOM 操作的编译器</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 13:34 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49542123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49542123' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49542123'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vidact compiles React components into direct DOM manipulation, bypassing the virtual DOM layer. This approach can reduce runtime overhead and open up new performance opportunities for React apps by emitting imperative updates.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Vidact 将 React 组件编译为直接的 DOM 操作，绕过虚拟 DOM。通过生成命令式的 DOM 更新，这种做法有望降低运行时开销，为 React 应用带来新的性能机会。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.vidact.dev/logo-64-dark.png' data-full='https://www.vidact.dev/logo-64-dark.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 13:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 62</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49541754'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Releasing the original Altair BASIC source code provides a window into early microcomputer software engineering and Microsoft&#x27;s roots. The document showcases tiny, hand-optimized code and the constraints of 1970s hardware, offering lessons for language design and open-source retrospectives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>公开原始的 Altair BASIC 源代码，为早期微型计算机软件工程与微软起源提供一扇窗。文档展示了极小且经过手工优化的代码，以及 1970 年代硬件条件下的设计取舍，为语言设计与开源史提供珍贵素材。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49542471' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49542471' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(348) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.ydb.tech/is-there-i-o-after-death-what-happens-to-io-uring-when-a-process-dies-92c65354873f?postPublishedType=repub' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Is There I/O After Death? What Happens to Io_uring When a Process Dies</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>进程终止后还有 I/O 吗？Io_uring 的行为</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 13:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49542471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49542471' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 27</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49542471'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Is there I/O after death? The article investigates how io_uring behaves when a process dies, including how outstanding operations are cleaned up and potential resource leaks. The discussion helps developers write robust lifecycle and error-handling code. Practical takeaways include ensuring proper cancellation and draining of queues.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文探讨进程死亡时 io_uring 的处理，包括未完成操作的清理以及可能的资源泄漏。为开发者提供了更健壮的生命周期与错误处理建议，着重在正确取消和清空队列的实用要点。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*vFOLC6WtniD-g-vyFU7coA.png' data-full='https://miro.medium.com/v2/resize:fit:1200/1*vFOLC6WtniD-g-vyFU7coA.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(350) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ucsfhealthcardiology.ucsf.edu/facstaff/spotlight/dr-melvin-scheinman-40th-anniversary-catheter-ablation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dr. Melvin Scheinman: 40th Anniversary of Catheter Ablation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>梅尔文·Scheinman博士：心导管消融术四十周年纪念</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 29, 2026 / 09:52 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49491398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 60</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49491398' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Dr. Melvin Scheinman’s work helped establish catheter ablation, now marking its 40th anniversary. The milestone underscores a pivotal shift in arrhythmia treatment and patient outcomes. Ongoing refinements in techniques continue to expand its safety and applicability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>梅尔文·Scheinman 博士的贡献推动了导管消融术的发展，如今迎来40周年。该里程碑凸显了在心律失常治疗和患者结局方面的关键转变。相关技术的持续改进正在扩展其安全性和适用性。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bayeux tapestry offers a richly encoded medieval chronicle with mysteries about its creation, stitching, and conservation. The piece draws lessons for modern data provenance, media curation, and collaborative storytelling. It highlights how physical artifacts inform digital understanding of history.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>巴约挂毯作为中世纪编年史的丰富编码对象，仍有关于创作、缝制及保存的诸多未解之谜。文章从中提炼出现代数据溯源、媒体保护及协作叙事的启示，强调实体遗产对数字史学的借鉴意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(356) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dub.uu.nl/en/column/pencil-case-model-creativity' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The pencil case model of creativity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>创造力的铅盒模型</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 05:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585703' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49585703'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The pencil-case model argues creativity emerges from recombining a fixed set of tools and ideas rather than sudden flashes of inspiration. It reframes how engineers brainstorm, prototype, and iterate on new products. Practitioners can apply it to improve ideation and collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>铅盒模型认为创造力来自于对有限工具和概念的重新组合，而非一瞬的灵感闪现。它重新定义工程师的头脑风暴、原型设计与迭代过程，帮助团队提升创意产生与协作效率。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49521590' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(359) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://andreasfertig.com/blog/2026/09/move-in-cpp-without-a-stdmove/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Move in C++ without a std:move</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 C++ 中无需 std::move 就能实现对象移动</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 06:17 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49521590' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49521590'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise guide shows patterns to achieve move semantics in C++ without explicitly calling std::move, relying on implicit moves, return value optimization, and careful type design. The piece helps you write more efficient code while avoiding common pitfalls around unintended copies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章展示了在不用显式调用 std::move 的情况下实现移动语义的模式，依赖隐式移动、拷贝省略等技术。对于编写高效 C++ 代码、避免不必要拷贝，同时避免常见错误，提供了实用的设计思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://andreasfertig.com/img/2026-09-01-move-without-move-li.png' data-full='https://andreasfertig.com/img/2026-09-01-move-without-move-li.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49537340' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(360) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ipv6.hanazo.no/posts/anniversary-personal/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Highest Point in the Netherlands</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>荷兰的最高点（最高点探访）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 07:58 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 58</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49537340' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49537340'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post surveys the Netherlands&#x27; highest point, typically the Vaalserberg, and reflects on its geography and accessibility. It offers a concise look at a geographic curiosity and the landscape around the border region.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(369) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/RCYF2rW8wgusidZk7/what-is-neuralese-and-why-is-it-bad' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is Nueralese and Why is it Bad</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>神经语言 Neuralese 及其为何有问题</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 18:33 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49559451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49559451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49559451'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Neuralese refers to the emergent internal language used by AI models, whose opacity makes debugging and alignment harder. The piece argues this secrecy raises risks of hidden behavior and misalignment. It advocates for interpretable interfaces and safer design practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>神经语言 Neuralese 指的是 AI 模型内部自发形成的语言，其不透明性让调试和对齐变得困难。文章指出这类秘密性带来隐藏行为与对齐偏差的风险，并倡导可解释的接口与更安全的设计方法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 09:14 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49538490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49538490' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 29</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49538490'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Evaluates nine harnesses for the same model and shows wide variation in per-pass cost, driven by runtime choices, hardware, and optimization. Important takeaway for cost-conscious deployment strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对同一模型的九种 harness 进行评测，展示每轮前向成本的巨大差异，受运行环境、硬件和优化策略影响。对成本敏感的部署制定了关键参考。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49505306' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(371) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Parque_Arqueol%C3%B3gico_do_Solst%C3%ADcio' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parque Arqueológico do Solstício</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊 Stonehenge：Solstício 考古公园</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 20:37 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49505306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49505306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49505306'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon Stonehenge refers to a project or site focused on solstice-related archaeology within the Amazon context, highlighting the intersection of culture, history, and modern interpretation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>“亚马逊 Stonehenge” 是一个聚焦于极日与 solstice 相关考古的项目/地点，展示文化与历史在现代语境中的诠释。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49463231' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(372) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://studyfinds.com/what-2000-buried-underpants-revealed-about-where-soil-is-most-alive/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What 2000 Buried Underpants Revealed About Where Soil Is Most Alive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2000 条埋在地下的内裤揭示土壤最活跃的位置</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 27, 2026 / 04:47 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49463231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49463231' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49463231'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A quirky study used 2000 buried underwear to map soil microbial activity and life, revealing hotspots of soil biology affected by moisture, pH, and organic matter. The results shed light on soil health and land management implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这项有趣的研究通过埋藏2000条内裤来绘制土壤微生物活性图，揭示受水分、pH值与有机质影响的土壤生物热点。研究为土壤健康与土地管理提供洞见。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://studyfinds.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Buried-Underwear.jpg' data-full='https://studyfinds.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/Buried-Underwear.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49539805' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49539805' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(373) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/science-nature/earths-organisms-developed-via-evolution-some-theorists-wonder-what-if-the-entire-cosmos-did-too-180989338/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Earth&#x27;s organisms developed via evolution. What if the cosmos did, too?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>地球上的生物通过进化发展；如果整个宇宙也经历进化会怎样？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 10:46 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49539805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 54</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49539805' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49539805'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Smithsonian piece explores the speculative idea that cosmic evolution could shape the universe much like natural selection shapes life on Earth. It surveys theoretical frameworks linking physics, complexity, and information processing to potential cosmos-wide evolution. The article prompts readers to rethink evolutionary narratives beyond biology and consider the implications for astrobiology and cosmology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Smithsonian 的文章探讨一个富有猜想性的想法：宇宙本身是否也可能经历类似地球生命进化的过程。文章回顾了将物理学、复杂性与信息处理联系起来的理论框架，以及对整个宇宙层面进化的潜在影响。促使读者重新思考超越生物学的进化叙事，并思考对天体生物学与宇宙学的意义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/caoAxlTOLyoIxtlGTD-YZkM3bpU=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(521x516:522x517)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/11/48/11487635-0fac-4b64-baa7-f7f3cb941e79/image_1.png' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/caoAxlTOLyoIxtlGTD-YZkM3bpU=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(521x516:522x517)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/11/48/11487635-0fac-4b64-baa7-f7f3cb941e79/image_1.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(379) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Sep/2/claudes-new-system-prompt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude&#x27;s new system prompt doesn&#x27;t want to reproduce song lyrics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 的新系统提示不再复现歌词</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 03:32 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575143' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 53</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575143' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49575143'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude’s latest system prompt enforces copyright-aware boundaries, disallowing the reproduction of song lyrics. This affects developers building apps on Claude, prompting considerations about prompt design, licensing, and content-policy enforcement in AI systems. It signals a broader shift toward automated copyright safeguards in language models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 最新系统提示对歌词等受版权保护内容进行了限制，禁止复现歌词。这一变化影响使用 Claude 的开发者，促使对提示设计、许可与内容策略执行进行权衡，体现语言模型在版权保护方面的日益严格趋势。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49506148' data-tags='Science,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49506148' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/science/2026/aug/31/forbidden-planet-was-plutos-2006-demotion-a-big-mistake' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Since it was stripped of planetary status, Pluto’s defenders have been fighting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自冥王星被除名天体后，捍卫者仍在奋战</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 23:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49506148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49506148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 178</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49506148'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article revisits Pluto’s 2006 reclassification from planet to dwarf planet and the ongoing advocacy arguing the decision was a mistake. It reflects on how taxonomy debates persist in astronomy, shaping public understanding and scientific policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>回顾冥王星在2006年被重新归类为矮行星的事件，以及其支持者持续的抗辩。文章指出科学分类学的争论仍在，并影响公众认知与科学政策的走向。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/74c9b2ca9daf3a137a609dc9e2b186d95888bfc1/1077_1477_2968_2375/master/2968.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9c51882c8689ab550f76f4bf34d044af' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/74c9b2ca9daf3a137a609dc9e2b186d95888bfc1/1077_1477_2968_2375/master/2968.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=9c51882c8689ab550f76f4bf34d044af' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49464287' data-tags='Science,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49464287' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.johndcook.com/blog/2026/08/21/ancient-number-system/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How would you know whether an ancient culture had zero?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>你如何判断一个古代文化是否有零？</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 27, 2026 / 06:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49464287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49464287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49464287'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Discusses how historians infer the existence (or absence) of zero in ancient numeral systems, considering positional notation, counting schemes, and artifacts. Highlights methodological challenges in uncovering early zero concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨历史学家如何推断古代数字系统是否有零，以及在位置记数、计数方式和文物证据方面面临的困难。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49540090' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(382) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.wsu.edu/press-release/2026/08/20/researchers-develop-electronic-skin-for-prosthetics-to-sense-temperature-and-pressure/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Electronic skin for prosthetics to sense temperature and pressure</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于假肢的电子皮肤：感知温度与压力</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 11:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49540090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49540090'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers developed electronic skin for prosthetics that senses temperature and pressure, delivering richer tactile feedback. The advancement could improve dexterity and reduce phantom limb sensations, bringing prosthetic control closer to natural touch. Real-world adoption will hinge on durability and seamless nerve integration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员开发出用于假肢的电子皮肤，具备温度和压力传感能力，提供更丰富的触觉反馈。该进展有望提升假肢的灵活度并缓解幻肢痛，使控制更接近自然触感。实际落地将依赖耐用性与与神经系统的整合难易度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://wpcdn.web.wsu.edu/news/uploads/sites/2797/2026/08/LEAD.jpg' data-full='https://wpcdn.web.wsu.edu/news/uploads/sites/2797/2026/08/LEAD.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.dolthub.com/blog/2026-08-31-doltlite-beta/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DoltLite: A SQLite fork with Git-style version control, built with 2k agent PRs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DoltLite：带 Git 风格版本控制的 SQLite 派生版本，历经两千次代理 PR 打磨</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 18:25 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 51</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516848' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49516848'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DoltLite is a SQLite fork that adds Git-style version control, enabling history and collaboration for embedded data. Built from thousands of PRs, it demonstrates a modular approach to data versioning and reproducibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DoltLite 是一个带有 Git 风格版本控制的 SQLite 派生版本，通过大量的 PR 打磨，展示了在嵌入式场景中实现数据版本控制与协作的思路。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49541331' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(391) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.jordangoodman.xyz/the-post-ai-internet-doesnt-look-great/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Post-AI Internet Doesn&#x27;t Look Great</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>后AI互联网并不乐观</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 12:41 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49541331' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49541331'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An analysis of a web dominated by AI content and agents argues it could degrade signal quality, amplify misinformation, and complicate trust and provenance. It questions whether automation truly improves the long-term health of the internet.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文认为被 AI 内容和智能代理主导的互联网，可能降低信息质量、助长错误信息、并使信誉与来源追踪变得更加困难，进而影响互联网的健康生态。作者质疑自动化是否真的改善在线体验，还是在某些方面拉低了标准。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.jordangoodman.xyz/static/og-image.png' data-full='https://www.jordangoodman.xyz/static/og-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(393) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kale: A Transformation-Safe Spreadsheet System</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Kale：一种具备变换安全性的电子表格系统</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 18:42 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49516962'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kale is a spreadsheet system designed to preserve semantics under transformations, preserving formulas and data integrity as users edit and rearrange. It aims to prevent silent formula changes and enable reproducible transformations with stronger correctness guarantees.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kale 提供在进行编辑和重排数据时仍能保持公式与语义正确性的电子表格系统。它致力于避免对公式的无意修改，并实现可重现的变换与更强的正确性保障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49572875' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(396) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.coderabbit.ai/blog/gpt-6-astra-code-review-evaluation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-6 Astra in code review: Gains, privacy, and cost</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-6 Astra 在代码审查中的表现：收益、隐私与成本</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 04, 2026 / 20:38 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49572875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 48</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49572875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49572875'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra speeds up code-review with better triage and inline suggestions, helping engineers catch issues earlier. But it raises privacy concerns when proprietary code is processed by AI and adds cost considerations for large teams. The evaluation weighs gains against privacy and cost, guiding practical use in review workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GPT-6 Astra 在代码审查中提升分拣与内联建议的速度，帮助工程师更早发现问题；但将私有代码交给 AI 处理存在隐私风险，并对大规模团队的成本构成考量。评估从收益、隐私与成本三方面权衡，为审查工作流中的实际应用提供指引。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.coderabbit.ai/content/assets/gpt-6-astra/header.png' data-full='https://www.coderabbit.ai/content/assets/gpt-6-astra/header.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49547763' data-tags='Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49547763' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(402) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/sep/02/japan-new-speed-limit-30kmh-narrow-city-streets' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Japan halves speed limit to 30km/h on all narrow city streets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>日本在所有狭窄市区街道统一限速至30公里/小时</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 02:21 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49547763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49547763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49547763'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Japan is cutting the speed limit to 30 km/h on all narrow city streets to improve pedestrian safety and overall urban livability. The move aligns with global trends toward slower, safer streets and could impact travel times, enforcement, and urban design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>日本将狭窄市区街道的限速统一降至每小时30公里，以提升行人安全和城市宜居性。这一举措与全球推动更慢更安全街区的趋势相契合，可能影响通勤时间、执法方式以及城市设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/239bad6d4d630d5c23de374c64025bb65a23c595/0_1123_3620_2894/master/3620.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=6e438e58aef117f560c2c3625f31545b' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/239bad6d4d630d5c23de374c64025bb65a23c595/0_1123_3620_2894/master/3620.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=6e438e58aef117f560c2c3625f31545b' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49555114' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49555114' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.publicbooks.org/every-novel-is-boring-until-it-isnt/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every Novel Is Boring–Until It Isn&#x27;t</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每部小说都很无聊，直到它不再无聊</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 03, 2026 / 12:10 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49555114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49555114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49555114'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A literary essay arguing that novels often feel tedious at first, but moments of wonder emerge through structure, voice, and surprise. It examines how readers&#x27; attention and context transform perceived dullness.</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不仅是 LG 电视在监视你：隐私与智能设备</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 03:36 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49575176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A video exposing data collection by smart TVs and other connected devices, detailing what data is gathered and how to protect privacy. It covers app permissions, default telemetry, and practical steps like network segmentation and opting out. It underscores the ongoing balance between convenience and surveillance in consumer tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>视频揭示智能电视及其他连网设备的数据收集情况，解释收集了哪些数据以及如何保护隐私。内容涵盖应用权限、默认遥测设置以及通过网络分段和关闭数据收集等方式的实用对策，强调了便利性与隐私之间的持续博弈。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49474515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49474515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49474515'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into timecode synchronization, SMPTE standards, and their practical use in film, video, and live productions. It covers how timecode coordinates multi-camera shoots and post-production workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>深入解读时间码与 SMPTE 标准，以及在影视与现场制作中的实际应用，如何用于多机位拍摄的同步与后期工作流。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49450461' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49450461' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://solar.lowtechmagazine.com/2011/05/bike-powered-electricity-generators-are-not-sustainable/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Commercially Available Bike Generators Are Not Sustainable (2011)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>市售自行车发电机并非可持续</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 26, 2026 / 08:04 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49450461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 43</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49450461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 40</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49450461'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical teardown of commercially available bike generators, arguing they are not a sustainable energy solution due to user effort, limited output, and lifecycle costs. The piece encourages focusing on real-world energy systems and efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对市售自行车发电机进行批判性评估，认为作为能源解决方案并不可持续，因为需要大量用户体力投入、产出有限及寿命成本高。文章倡导把注意力放在现实可行的能源系统与效率提升上。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://solar.lowtechmagazine.com/2011/05/bike-powered-electricity-generators-are-not-sustainable/images/dithers/pedal-powered-generator_dithered.png' data-full='https://solar.lowtechmagazine.com/2011/05/bike-powered-electricity-generators-are-not-sustainable/images/dithers/pedal-powered-generator_dithered.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 20:53 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49545747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49545747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49545747'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Dutch central bank moved 86 tonnes of gold from the US due to geopolitical unrest, illustrating how reserve diversification and security concerns shape central-bank decisions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>荷兰央行因地缘政治动荡将86吨黄金从美国运回，凸显央行在储备多元化与安全性方面的考量。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/the-forgotten-history-of-small-nuclear-reactors' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Forgotten History of Small Nuclear Reactors (2015)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>小型核反应堆的被遗忘历史（2015）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 28, 2026 / 15:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49484796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 42</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49484796' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49484796'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article revisits the rise and fall of small modular reactors, tracing policy debates, safety concerns, and economic challenges that limited early deployment. It explains why modular reactor concepts persist and what they could mean for future energy grids.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章回顾小型模块化核反应堆的兴衰，梳理政策辩论、安全性和经济挑战为何限制早期部署，并讨论这类概念为何仍具潜力以及对未来电网的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/the-enrico-fermi-atomic-power-plant-unit-1-in-newport-mich-was-an-early-small-nuclear-reactor-constructed-with-funding-from.jpg?id=25577756&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C77%2C0%2C78' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/the-enrico-fermi-atomic-power-plant-unit-1-in-newport-mich-was-an-early-small-nuclear-reactor-constructed-with-funding-from.jpg?id=25577756&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C77%2C0%2C78' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49441609'>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Polishing Cloth 的相关更新带来了对材料与使用指南的修订，回应用户反馈并提升使用中的安全性。此次更新体现了在现实使用场景中对消费级配件信息披露与安全性的关注。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.governance.fyi/p/accidental-aesthetics-and-romance' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Accidental Aesthetics and Romance of Power Wires</a></p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 30, 2026 / 16:00 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过以高压线等基础设施为切入点，探讨外观与设计如何揭示权力关系，以及它们在治理与社会生活中的作用。</p>
