<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49589768' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://d2lang.com/blog/d2-non-profit/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>D2 Is Non-Profit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>D2：非盈利项目</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 12:11 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49589768' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49589768'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>D2, a programming language, declares itself non-profit, shifting governance and development to a community-driven model. The move signals a focus on sustainability and openness over commercial aims. It invites contributors to shape the language&#x27;s trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>D2 作为一种编程语言宣布自己为非盈利项目，将治理与开发带入社区驱动模式。此举强调可持续性与开放性优先于商业目标，期待社区贡献共同推动语言发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2lang.com/img/opengraph.png' data-full='https://d2lang.com/img/opengraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49516047' data-tags='Legal,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49516047' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://itp.cdn.icann.org/en/files/consensus-policies/rsep-2026013-name-request-15-04-2026-en.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Discontinuation of third level domain registrations for the .name TLD [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>.name 顶级域名将停止注册三级域名（PDF）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 16:24 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49516047' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 30</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49516047'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ICANN policy document outlines ending third-level registrations for the .name domain, affecting personal branding formats like first.last.name. It signals registration policy changes and potential migration needs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ICANN 的政策文件宣布停止 .name 域名的三级域名注册，影响如 name 作为个人品牌的用法，并提示注册规则的变动和潜在的迁移需求。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49494236' data-tags='Open Source,Science,Health,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49494236' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mnh-jansson/open-battery-information' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Open Battery Information</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>开放式电池信息</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 29, 2026 / 16:27 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49494236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49494236' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49494236'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Open Battery Information is a community-driven project that standardizes battery metadata—chemistry, health, and performance—via open specifications. This openness helps cross-device interoperability and transparency across consumer electronics and energy storage.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>开放式电池信息是一个以社区为驱动的项目，利用开放规范统一电池元数据（化学成分、健康状态、性能等）。开放的数据有助于实现跨设备互操作性与能源存储领域的透明度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d20b717dd24d321c4f4251ea7c2fd8fc27be04b776ebf1980d470b6f1288b3bb/mnh-jansson/open-battery-information' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/d20b717dd24d321c4f4251ea7c2fd8fc27be04b776ebf1980d470b6f1288b3bb/mnh-jansson/open-battery-information' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49534025' data-tags='Hardware,Programming,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49534025' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(425) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.crowdsupply.com/sutajio-kosagi/fomu' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fomu An FPGA board that fits inside your USB port</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fomu：一个能塞进 USB 端口的 FPGA 板</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 02:48 PT</span></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fomu is an FPGA board designed to fit inside a USB port, enabling ultra‑compact prototyping and on‑the‑go hardware experimentation. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fomu 是一个可以塞进 USB 端口的 FPGA 板，适合极简化的原型开发和便携式硬件实验，体现了 FPGA 开发板在体积缩小方面的进展以及教育友好型项目的潜力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 26, 2026 / 05:40 PT</span></p>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49448019'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fixed specifications are often unrealistic in complex software; the piece advocates iterative design, testing, and robust verification (or contracts) to cope with ambiguity. It discusses practical methods for building reliable systems in uncertain environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在复杂软件中，固定的规格往往难以实现。文章倡导迭代设计、测试与鲁棒验证（或契约）来应对不确定性，提供在不确定环境中构建可靠系统的实用方法。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49526210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 40</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49526210' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49526210'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores practical patterns for lazy evaluation in Python, focusing on deferring imports and computations to speed startup and reduce memory usage. The post also highlights tooling support, like a flake8 plugin, to safely enforce lazy patterns without breaking code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨在 Python 中实现惰性加载的实用模式，重点是推迟导入与计算以提升启动速度和降低内存占用。文中还介绍了工具支持，如 flake8 插件，用于安全地强制实施惰性模式而不破坏现有代码。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49587285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49587285' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 36</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>阿尔伯塔省对太阳能板征收惩罚性税，显示出对可再生能源的政策转向，可能减缓该省的太阳能普及。此举引发对激励政策、能源政策与本地太阳能经济性的讨论，批评者担忧会拖累气候目标与电网韧性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mathenchant.wordpress.com/2026/08/19/solving-the-flat-cube/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Solving the Flat Cube</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>解平面立方</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Aug 31, 2026 / 05:02 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49508619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49508619' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49508619'>

<p class='hn-title'>(449) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/opinions/windows-11s-special-developer-edition-sounds-like-yet-another-marketing-misfire/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Windows 11&#x27;s &quot;special&quot; developer edition looks like another marketing misfire</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Windows 11 的特别开发者版看起来又是一场营销失策</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 14:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49591036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49591036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49591036'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Windows 11 &#x27;special&#x27; developer edition appears to be more marketing than meaningful tooling, with critics saying it adds little practical value for developers. The release underscores ongoing questions about how Microsoft positions developer tooling. In short, another branding misfire rather than a substantive upgrade.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Windows 11 的“特别”开发者版似乎更多是市场宣传而非实际工具，批评者表示对开发者帮助有限。该版本再次凸显微软在开发者工具定位上的持续争议，简言之并非一次实质性的升级。</p>
</div>
</div>

</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49541025'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The PHP Foundation argues that digital sovereignty is achievable through PHP-based ecosystems, highlighting governance, portability, and open standards. The piece frames PHP as a practical platform for building sovereign web apps and hosting environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>PHP 基金会声称数字主权可以通过基于 PHP 的生态系统实现，强调治理、可移植性与开放标准的重要性。该观点将 PHP 视为构建自主网络应用与本地托管的实际平台。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全合一 Neo6502kbd 6502 计算机（含键盘与 USB 端口）</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 04:51 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49585605' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 16</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49585605'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A new open-source Neo6502 variant combines a 6502-based computer with an integrated keyboard, USB hosts, a power switch, and USB-C for power and programming. It’s a compact, all-in-one retro development machine that appeals to hobbyists and retro-computing fans. Availability and documentation will determine its adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一款开源的 Neo6502kbd 变体把 6502 处理器的计算机与内置键盘、USB 主机、开关及 USB-C 电源/编程端口整合为一体，是面向爱好者的迷你复古开发机。未来的普及取决于文档与可用性。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49477925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49477925' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FF-16-TUI introduces a terminal-based interface for interactive pattern discovery in binaries, lowering the barrier for reverse engineers and security researchers to explore patterns without heavy GUI tools.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FF-16-TUI 提供一个基于终端的交互界面，用于在二进制文件中发现模式，降低了逆向分析和安全研究的门槛，便于在无需重型 GUI 的情况下进行探索性工作。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(458) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mceglowski.substack.com/p/icy-moons-are-ocean-worlds' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Icy Moons Are Ocean Worlds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>冰冷的月球其实是海洋世界</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 06, 2026 / 06:07 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49586207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49586207'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Icy moons are ocean worlds beneath icy crusts, with subsurface oceans likely driving geology and potential habitats. The piece explains why these moons are prime targets for future missions and astrobiology. It underscores the implications for life beyond Earth and exploration tech.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>冰冷的卫星在冰壳下可能拥有地下海洋，是天体生物学的重要目标。文章解释了为何这些月球成为未来探测与研究的重点对象，强调了寻找地外生命的可能性及相关探测技术的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>明年将迎来‘世纪日食’，西班牙、埃及等地进入日全食带</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 02, 2026 / 06:08 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49535634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49535634' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 39</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A rare solar eclipse dubbed the &#x27;Eclipse of the Century&#x27; will sweep across regions including Spain and Egypt next year. Scientists and travelers are preparing for a multi-country viewing event that showcases orbital geometry and offers a global science outreach moment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>罕见的“世纪日食”将于明年横跨西班牙、埃及等地区，进入日全食带。科学家与旅行者正在为跨国观测做准备，这一天象现象生动展示了轨道几何并为全球科普与旅游提供了契机。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 12:59 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49527287' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EU repair directives require easier repairability, spare parts access, and longer product lifecycles; this forces hardware designers to rethink modularity, serviceability, and business models. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧盟的维修指令要求产品更易修理、可获得备件且使用寿命更长，正在推动硬件设计在模块化、可维护性与商业模式上的重新考量。文章解释了这些政策如何改变硬件设计以及对消费者权利的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 14:43 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580980' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 17</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580980'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York City and Los Angeles school districts pause using AI tools in classrooms or district operations to assess safety, bias, and governance implications. This sets a high-profile precedent for AI policy in education.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国最大的两大学区暂停在课堂和行政工作中使用 AI 工具，以评估安全性、偏见与治理影响。这为教育领域的 AI 政策定下显著的先例。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49471950' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49471950' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(468) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/why-are-rivers-so-mathematical-20260810/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why are rivers so mathematical?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>河流为何如此具有数学性？</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49521631'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ExactTex is a gradually-typed superset of LaTeX that adds type annotations to macros and content, enabling stronger tooling and early error detection in LaTeX documents. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ExactTex 是 LaTeX 的渐进类型化超集，为宏和内容添加类型注解，以提升工具链的健壮性与早期错误检测。目标是在复杂排版场景中提高可靠性和开发效率。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49528484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 18</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49528484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post argues for a practical approach to comparing solo PQ schemes against ECC+PQ hybrids, pointing out common bugs and misinterpretations. It offers guidelines to ensure fair, apples-to-apples crypto comparisons in security and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提出一种实用方法，用于将 solo PQ 算法与 ECC+PQ 混合方案进行对比，指出常见的实现错误与解读偏差，并给出在安全性与性能评估中的公平对比指南。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49522194' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(486) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1111/pala.70064' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Praearcturus gigas: giant scorpion from Lower Devonian (Lochkovian) of Britain</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Praearcturus gigas：英国下奥陶纪的巨型蝎化石</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 01, 2026 / 07:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49522194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 16</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49522194' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49522194'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Praearcturus gigas is a giant scorpion from the Lochkovian stage of the Lower Devonian in Britain, shedding light on early arthropod gigantism and predator-prey dynamics. The fossil expands our view of Devonian ecosystems and arthropod evolution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Praearcturus gigas 是英国下奥陶纪 Lochkovian 阶段的巨大蝎化石，揭示早期节肢动物的巨大体型及捕食-被捕关系。该发现拓展了对晚古生代生态系统与节肢动物演化的理解。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49576156' data-tags='Web,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(487) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.infoq.com/news/2026/09/jquery-20-years/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Twenty Years of jQuery: How a Little Library Rewired Web Development</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>二十年 jQuery：一个小库如何重塑 Web 开发</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 06:06 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49576156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 15</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49576156' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49576156'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two decades after its debut, jQuery&#x27;s influence on simplifying DOM manipulation, cross-browser compatibility, and plugin ecosystems remains evident, even as newer frameworks rise. The piece reflects on how jQuery shaped developer ergonomics and expectations, and why its prominence has waned with modern tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在诞生二十年后，jQuery 对简化 DOM 操作、跨浏览器兼容性和插件生态的影响依然明显，尽管新框架层出不穷。文章回顾了它如何塑造开发者的工作流程与期望，以及在现代工具兴起后为何影响力有所下降。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.infoq.com/news/2026/09/jquery-20-years/en/headerimage/generatedHeaderImage-1788335247571.jpg' data-full='https://res.infoq.com/news/2026/09/jquery-20-years/en/headerimage/generatedHeaderImage-1788335247571.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/h4ckf0r0day/obscura' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Obscura: Native rendering is here. No Chromium required</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Obscura：原生渲染已到来，无需 Chromium</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 14:16 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580771' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580771'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Obscura now supports native rendering, removing the need for Chromium. This promises privacy gains, lower memory usage, and potential performance improvements for a lightweight, open-source browser.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宣布 Obscura 已实现原生渲染，摆脱对 Chromium 的依赖。这一改动可能带来隐私保护、内存占用与性能方面的提升，推动这款轻量开源浏览器的发展。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/536bced54bec43025a111a53044d709b914c7fd4234752ce798e9468d91cfbec/h4ckf0r0day/obscura' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/536bced54bec43025a111a53044d709b914c7fd4234752ce798e9468d91cfbec/h4ckf0r0day/obscura' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49580687' data-tags='AI,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49580687' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(489) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/leaders/2026/08/06/how-ai-is-breaking-the-british-state' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How AI is breaking the British state</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人工智能如何撼动英国政府体系</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 14:05 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49580687' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49580687'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI is reshaping public services, governance, and policy in Britain, from welfare to border control, highlighting governance gaps and the need for new skills and oversight. The piece argues for thoughtful integration and robust public-sector AI strategy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>人工智能正在重塑英国公共服务、治理与政策，从福利体系到边境管理都在受影响，暴露治理缺口并强调对新技能与监督的需求。倡导审慎的 AI 融合和健全的公共部门 AI 策略。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49579024' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<div class='hn-digest-summary-header' role='button' tabindex='0' aria-expanded='true' aria-controls='digest-details-49579024' aria-label='Hide summary and image'>
<p class='hn-title'>(490) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/world/2026/aug/10/dogs-distinguish-fearful-angry-sad-human-faces-brain-scans' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dogs can distinguish between fearful and angry or sad human faces</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>狗狗能分辨恐惧、愤怒与悲伤的人脸</p>
<p class='hn-meta2'><span class='hn-meta2-created'>Created: Sep 05, 2026 / 11:00 PT</span></p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49579024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 9</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49579024' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
</div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' id='digest-details-49579024'>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research shows dogs can distinguish between fearful faces and angry or sad expressions in humans, suggesting nuanced emotion processing. Brain scans reveal distinct neural responses to different emotions, underscoring dogs&#x27; social cognition and implications for training and welfare.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示狗狗能分辨人类的恐惧面孔与愤怒或悲伤表情，表明它们具备更细致的情绪处理能力。脑部扫描揭示对不同情绪的神经反应差异，凸显狗对人类情绪的社会认知，并对训练与福利具有潜在意义。</p>
